## Document 021: 20260914_投資週報_地緣風險升溫物價維持高檔， 市場聚焦Fed會議

Report source: Yuanta (投資週報)
Date: 2026-09-14
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同
意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 9 月 14 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國 8 月 CPI 公布後，市場對聯準會政策路徑的關注進一步升溫：美伊衝突持續升溫帶動市
場對原油供給的擔憂，推升國際油價維持高檔震盪，能源價格上漲也使通膨再度成為市場關
注焦點。美國 8 月 CPI 年增率為 3.4%，與前月持平，但月增率由 0.1%升至 0.4%；核心 CPI
年增率則降至 2.4%。此次數據顯示通膨回落進程並未逆轉，但能源成本攀升仍可能增加未來
物價壓力。儘管聯準會理事表示，若通膨維持改善趨勢，9 月傾向按兵不動，但油價上漲是

否影響後續降通膨進程仍有待觀察，使利率政策路徑與市場預期持續出現分歧。
◼ AI 基建需求持續擴張，台灣 8 月出口再創歷史新高：台灣 8 月出口達 824 億美元、年增
41%，刷新歷史紀錄，其中電子零組件出口首度突破 300 億美元，年增 58%。AI 伺服器、
高階晶片、資料中心設備與雲端建設需求持續強勁，成為出口與科技產業成長的核心動能。
◼ 胡賽武裝威脅帶動西德州油價突破 100 美元大關：胡賽武裝 9 月 8 日攻擊沙國能源設施，9
月 10 日再奪取鄰近曼德海峽的摩卡港，推升能源風險溢價。由於沙國已將部分原油改經紅海
延布港出口，亞洲航線仍須通過曼德海峽。胡賽勢力逼近關鍵航道，即使尚未封鎖海峽，也可
能降低航商與保險業者承運意願，增添紅海供應中斷疑慮，帶動西德州油價突破 100 美元。
市場展望
◼ AI 題材擴散，科技股中期向上趨勢料不變：近期股市修正主要來自美債殖利率攀升壓縮高評
價科技股現值，半導體族群股價表現承壓；然而，AI 變現題材接棒，資金由晶片端轉向軟體應
用層，預料 AI 應用題材將持續發酵。建議降低高評價、高擁擠與高度依賴遠期獲利族群的集

中度，此外，投資人可採取定期定額或是趁股價拉回時分批佈局具獲利潛力的台日股市。
◼ 3～10 年期美國公債與投資級債為債市首選：美國財政部擴大長天期公債回購 ， 一度帶動長端
殖利率回落，但通膨與需求數據仍具韌性，且就業市場表現穩健，使年底前市場升息預期再度
升溫。市場對美國財政赤字、公債供給及長期通膨仍要求較高風險補償，考量當前 3～10 年
期美國公債與投資級債能鎖定目前較高息收 ， 且其受長端財政風險影響也相對較小 ， 故現階段
3～10 年期美國公債及投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

市場焦點
聯準會9月
升息一碼
• 聯邦基準利率升至 3.75%~4.00%，經濟預測部分上調2026-2028年GDP成長率、失業率皆下
調，通膨降溫進程仍相對緩慢 ，點陣圖顯示今年還有升息一碼的機會 ，這次升息對股票而言 ，
聯準會沒有準備以大幅犧牲經濟與就業換取通膨下降 ，降低了硬著陸風險；對債券而言 ，短
端仍須消化更高的政策利率，但長端反而可能受惠於抗通膨可信度改善。
AI模型
開發放緩
• Anthropic、OpenAI及xAI相繼支持放緩最先進AI模型開發速度。不過，前沿模型開發速度放
緩，並不代表AI基礎建設需求同步轉弱。晶片、算力、資料中心及能源等AI基礎建設仍處於供
不應求格局 ，短期若因市場情緒或資金槓桿調整而出現回檔 ，較可能反映估值與持倉修正 ，

而非AI需求基本面反轉 。後續若前期累積的槓桿壓力逐步消化 ，且全球總體經濟及地緣政治
風險未進一步惡化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值。
台灣央行
• 利率決議重點： (1)台灣央行宣布基準利率維持不變 ，重貼現率、擔保放款融通利率和短期融
通利率分別維持2%、2.375%和4.25%。(2)放鬆房市信用管制 ，調整自然人第2戶購屋貸款最
高成數上限，由6成調升為7成。(3)上修今年經濟成長率及通膨率 ，GDP上修至11.48%、CPI
及核心CPI分別上修至2.03%及2.16。(4)央行預估明年AI基礎建設需求將持續貢獻台灣出口表
現，不過由於基期已墊高，預測明年台灣經濟成長率為5.82%。

資料來源：Fed，華爾街見聞，2026.9.17
貨幣決議與9月、7月聲明稿差異，不再強調能源衝擊 全員支持升息
• 聯準會9月升息一碼，聯邦基準利率在3.75%~4.00%。
聲明稿重點差異
紅色字體為9月的新增部分，括號內藍色字體為刪除的7月聲明措辭
FOMC以12票贊成升息一碼 批准發布以下聲明 ：委員會決定 ，將聯邦基金利率目標 區間上調至 3.75%至4%維持在
3.50%至3.75%，以支持聯準會的雙重使命。委員會延續其維持銀行體系充足準備金的政策。
儘管中東衝突在一定程度上帶來了高水準的不確定性 ，美國經濟活動持續以穩健步調擴張 。儘管地緣政治情勢 等因素
使經濟前景仍籠 罩高度不確定性，但國內支出保持韌性，生產力成長強勁，企業資本投資也相當強勁穩健。新增就業

人數大致跟上勞動力成長速度，失業率也變化不大，顯示就業市場目前仍大致維持穩定。
相較於委員會2%的目標，通貨膨脹率依然偏高 ，這在一定程度上反映了部分特定部門 (包括能源)因供給衝擊所導致的
價格上漲。今日的政策行動，將有助於讓通膨更及時地回到2%目標，委員會將實現價格穩定。

聯準會會後記者會問答重點整理
1
通膨「過高且持續太久」 當
前重心偏向物價穩定
通膨數據未改善，通膨過高且持續太久，聯準會當前的政策重心偏向雙重使命中的物價穩定 。透過政策
行動表明，聯準會對抑制通膨是認真的。
2
經濟、就業與地緣政治促成
一致升息
近幾週公布的數據證實美國經濟正在走強 ，基礎經濟成長率高於先前判斷；其次 ，勞動市場依然穩健；
第三，通膨持續高於目標，地緣政治局勢也改變經濟展望 。華許表示，這三項因素共同促成當天「堅定
且一致」的決定。
3
點狀圖暗示今年再升 1碼 但
不是政策承諾
最新經濟預測顯示，多數官員認為今年仍有進一步升息空間。12 名官員預期年底前再升息 1 碼，另有 4
人預期還會升息 2 碼，僅 2 人主張利率維持在目前水準。不過，點狀圖反映的是官員對適當利率路徑的

個別預測，並非正式的承諾。
4
拒絕提供前瞻指引 不預判下
次會議結果
面對下一次升息可能落在 10 月或 12 月的追問，華許表示「我不給出前瞻指引。」
他強調，先前公開表達的是對貨幣政策紀律的承諾 ，而不是對特定利率決策的承諾 。聯準會不會預判未
來任何會議結果，後續行動仍將依據最新經濟數據及展望作出決定。
5
升息非市場逼迫 迴避川普施
壓問題
面對此次升息是否由債市波動及市場壓力所推動的提問，華許予以否認。
他表示是根據就業走勢、經濟強度及整體情勢作出決定 ，市場價格可作為觀察依據，但不能決定聯準會
的政策。
資料來源：Fed，華爾街見聞，2026.9.17

市場反應與投資建議
9/16
市場反應
• 市場將華許記者會解讀為偏鷹，那斯達克指數、標普
500指數、道瓊工業指數皆下跌。
• 10 年 期 美 債 殖 利 率 在 利 率 決 議 公 布 後 一 度 降 至
4.9323%，隨後回升至接近 4.99%，在關鍵的5%關
口附近波動。
• 2年期殖利率則由盤中低點4.5975%升至4.7124%，
反映投資人重新提高對後續升息的預期。
• 黃金跌破4,260美元。
• 利率市場顯示 ，交易員加大 聯準會在2026 年底前進
一步升息的押注，12月升息機率升至約50%。

台灣利率連10凍 明年經濟展望佳
• 利率決議重點： (1)台灣央行宣布基準利率維持不變 ，重貼現率、擔保放款融通利率和短期融通利率分別維持 2%、2.375%
和4.25%。(2)放鬆房市信用管制，調整自然人第2戶購屋貸款最高成數上限，由6成調升為7成。(3)上修今年經濟成長率及通
膨率，GDP上修至11.48%(六月預估9.45 %)、CPI及核心CPI分別上修至2.03%及2.16%(六月預估1.91%及1.90%)。
• 觀點：全球AI資本支出維持上調的趨勢，台股供應鏈訂單直接受惠，央行預估明年AI基礎建設需求將持續貢獻台灣出口表現，
不過由於基期已墊高，預測明年台灣經濟成長率為5.82%。
• 台股短線展望：上週隨著聯準會利率決議公布後，台股量能回升，顯示短線消息面或已利空出盡。權值股台積電領軍台灣加

權指數站上月線，技術指標偏正向。投資佈局方面，由於地緣政治、通膨及貿易關稅風險仍存在不確定性，建議AI概念持股
核心布局行業龍頭企業，留意中小型成長股較易受到外部因素而股價大幅波動。

美國零售強勁回升擴大內需支撐，惟價格壓力與升息提高市場波動
• 零售全面回升，消費動能展現廣度：美國8月零售銷售月增1.2%，創3月以來最大增幅，優於市場預期，並扭轉7月修正
後月減0.5%；非實體店面銷售月增 2.6%、電子及家電增 1.6%，餐飲服務亦增 1.2%，且扣除汽車與汽油後仍增 1.2%，
控制組更增1.4%，顯示消費回升並非僅由汽車與燃料支出帶動，第三季內需仍具支撐。
• 名目消費偏強，通膨與升息增添需求壓力： 不過8月零售銷售年增6.0%屬名目增幅，部分反映物價上升因素；同期 CPI
年增3.4%，汽油價格月增3.9%，能源成本上升可能壓縮家庭實質購買力 ，加上Fed於9月決議升息1碼，後續利率政策
仍將視通膨與經濟數據調整，使市場焦點由消費回升轉向評估高利率與高物價對後續需求動能的影響。

• 後市觀察：短期零售回升仍有助支撐美國景氣，但若能源成本持續走高，加上升息後借貸成本上升，消費動能能否維持
將成為重要觀察點；投資配置可偏向美國投資級短中天期債券 ，兼顧收益與利率風險，股票則可聚焦獲利穩定、具定價
能力的大型優質企業，降低需求放緩與資金成本升高的影響。

顧 • AI安全監管升溫，短期壓抑市場情緒：Anthropic、OpenAI及xAI相繼支持放緩最先進AI模型開發速度，並引入獨立第三方
評估等安全機制，引發市場對AI資本支出降溫的疑慮。不過，前沿模型開發速度放緩，並不代表AI基礎建設需求同步轉弱。
Alphabet、微軟及亞馬遜近期AI相關積壓訂單持續攀升，合計達約1.64兆美元，其中微軟及Alphabet分別突破6,000億及
5,000億美元，搭配雲端業務持續成長，顯示企業AI需求仍具韌性，相關訂單有望逐步轉化為未來數年的營收與獲利。
• 槓桿壓力逐步消化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值：美國雲端巨頭擴大AI資本支出，壓抑短期自由現金流；相較之下，亞洲
科技企業自由現金流可望由目前約2,450億增至2028年的6,330億美元，支撐亞洲科技供應鏈中長期發展。短期若因市場情

緒或資金槓桿調整而出現回檔，較可能反映估值與持倉修正，而非AI需求基本面反轉。後續若前期累積的槓桿壓力逐步消化，
且全球總體經濟及地緣政治風險未進一步惡化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值。

油價在美國中期選舉前，或將維持在高檔
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.14
伊朗戰爭期間卡達油氣收入暴跌
卡達政府的石油和天然氣收入 美伊衝突
• 美伊衝突短期內仍難見明確解方，彭博預估雙方後續可能採取的行動，主要可分為以下四種情境：
(1)伊朗承受經濟封鎖：伊朗承受美國經濟制裁與施壓，避免進一步重大軍事升級。在美方護航行動支持下，商船仍可維持通行
荷姆茲海峽，國際原油供應未出現明顯中斷，油價預估在每桶80美元區間波動。
(2)伊朗將攻擊範圍限於海灣地區油輪與美軍：伊朗主要鎖定海灣地區能源運輸船隻及美軍目標，美方則採取有限度軍事回應，

衝突維持在一定範圍內，油價預估在每桶90美元區間波動。
(3)伊朗擴大攻擊範圍：攻擊目標不再局限於海灣地區船隻及美軍基地，海灣能源基礎設施以及紅海等重要航運咽喉，均可能成
為目標。局勢升級期間，油價可能維持在每桶100美元以上，待衝突緩和後回落至70至80美元區間，此情境發生的可能性最高。
(4)美伊雙方全面擴大開戰：伊朗進一步擴大攻擊目標，導致美國軍事資產及區域能源基礎設施遭受重大破壞，甚至不排除以色
列重新加入戰局，油價可能升至每桶120美元。

全球債市拋售勢頭不墜，AI融資競逐資金推升美債殖利率
• 長端殖利率走高，財政與通膨壓力仍是主因 ：近期美國長
天期公債殖利率持續走高，其中10年期美債殖利率更突破
5%，創2007年以來高點；除能源價格上升推升通膨疑慮
外，美國財政赤字、政府債務及持續性的發債需求 ，也提
高市場對長天期公債供給與期限溢酬的要求 ，反映長端利
率壓力仍未完全紓解。
• AI企業擴大融資，增加公司債供給與資金競逐： AI資料中
心、晶片與電力基礎建設的資本支出快速增加 ，帶動大型
科技企業提高債券融資規模；截至 6月，華爾街市場策略
研究機構Yardeni Research在追蹤外國私人投資人過去一
年買進美國公司債約3,900億美元，高於美國公債約3,290
億美元，顯示資金配置確有轉向公司債的現象 ，不過市場

研究亦指出，AI企業發債對美債殖利率的直接推升效果相
對有限，應可視為推動長天期利率上行的眾多因素之一。
• 後市觀察：若財政融資與AI基礎建設支出持續維持高檔 ，
長天期公債仍面臨較大的供給與資金配置壓力；但 AI投資
仍具中長期成長動能 ，配置上可關注資料中心 、半導體及
電力基礎建設供應鏈 ，固定收益則以短中天期美債及高品
質投資級公司債為主，降低長天期利率波動的影響。

新興市場本幣債券擁高票息提供防禦力
• 高票息提供防禦力：對債券投資人而言，票息就像安全墊。當市場利率上升、債券價格下跌時，持續收到的票息可以部分甚
至完全抵銷價格損失。新興市場本幣政府債券今年的表現，比市場原先預期更具韌性。目前新興市場本幣債券的持有收益約
為4.54%，已經足以對利率風險形成相當程度的保護 ，即使今年存續期間對多數市場造成負面影響 ，票息收益仍讓整體報酬
維持正數。這也是為什麼新興市場本幣債券在美伊衝突爆發後一度重挫超過 6%，但之後仍能逐步反彈 。哥倫比亞、巴西、
匈牙利及墨西哥的債券表現尤其突出。
• 市場已反映相當程度的升息風險：問題在於未來幾個月投資環境可能比上半年更加困難，能源價格上漲推升通膨，也迫使新
興市場央行維持較為鷹派的貨幣政策 。市場目前甚至預期 ，19個主要新興市場中 ，有18個市場的債券殖利率可能在年底前

上升。殖利率上升意味著債券價格承壓，因此投資人不能只看表面的高票息，必須同時評估未來利率變化可能造成資本損失。
新興市場本幣債券各因子對報酬率貢獻
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.15

顧
新興市場本幣債券目前最大風險在於利率與匯率
• 存續期間仍是今年最主要的拖累因素：一般而言，債券的存續期間越高，當殖利率上升時，債券價格下跌的幅度就可能越大。
今年19個符合基準指數條件的新興市場中 ，有14個市場的總報酬受到存續期間拖累 ，其中韓國、馬來西亞、印尼、泰國及
菲律賓受到的負面影響最為明顯。更值得注意的是，新興市場債券的利率表現與美國公債高度相關，因此，投資新興市場債
券，不能只觀察當地央行，也必須密切留意美國聯準會利率政策及美國公債殖利率走勢。
• 匯率可能成為下一個重要變數：新興市場本幣債券除了債券價格本身，還存在另一項重要風險：匯率。今年即期匯率變動反
而對新興市場本幣債券帶來正面貢獻 ，約增加57個基點。但如果能源價格持續上漲並形成滯脹環境 ，順周期貨幣可能面臨

更大的貶值壓力 。目前市場預期年底前新興市場貨幣整體仍偏弱 ，19種新興市場貨幣中 ，有11種預計兌美元貶值 。其中土
耳其、哥倫比亞及巴西貨幣的潛在跌幅相對較大。這代表即使債券本身提供不錯的票息，如果當地貨幣大幅貶值，換算成美
元或其他投資人本國貨幣後，仍可能侵蝕甚至吞噬債券收益。
• 小結與投資建議：目前新興市場本幣債券比較像是高票息換取較高波動的投資，而不是單純追求資本利得的交易。在殖利率
已處於相對高檔、但利率與匯率風險仍未完全解除的情況下，採取分散市場、控制部位、逢回布局的策略會比追高更為合適。
新興市場本幣債券存續期間的貢獻度
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.15

• 多重資產配置可有效風險管理：美伊衝突持續及聯準會利率政策，使今年金融市場震盪加劇，加上第四季美國期中選舉到來，
增加市場更多不確定性，不過從歷史經驗來看，地緣政治事件對股市的影響多屬短期現象，市場在初期情緒性下跌後，通常會
逐步回歸基本面，相較於預測事件發展，投資人更應透過多元資產配置分散風險，以提升投資組合的穩定性與長期抗震能力。
• 透過多重資產廣納收益來源：在利率維持相對高檔的環境下，投資人更應重視收益來源的多元化，從下圖可見，相較於過去幾
年，掩護性買權、非投資等級債，及投資等級債等資產的收益率均顯著提升，提供投資組合更具吸引力的現金流來源，其中掩
護性買權收益率已超過10%，非投資等級債，及投資等級債亦分別達7%及5%以上水準，透過結合不同收益型資產，不僅有助

提升整體收益潛力，更能降低對單一市場或利率變動的依賴，在市場波動加劇時提高投資組合的穩定度與韌性，兼顧收益與長
期成長目標。
布局全球多重資產，掌握成長契機
各類資產收益率(股息率或殖利率)
資料來源: 凱基投信 2026.8.1

前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 本資料所載內容如涉及商品資訊 ，商品相關資訊仍以經核可之條款及文宣 (DM)為主，本行網站已備有公開說明書可供查閱基金應負擔之費用及相關投資風險 。投
資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。

## Document 134: 9月PMI数据点评：生产、价格上行共振拉升PMI

Source PDF: all report pdf/9月PMI数据点评：生产、价格上行共振拉升PMI.pdf
Report source: 万联证券
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

[Table_RightTitle] 
宏观研究|宏观跟踪报告

[Table_Title] 
生产、价格上行共振拉升 PMI

[Table_ReportType] 
——9 月 PMI 数据点评

[Table_ReportDate] 2026 年 09 月 30 日

[T able_Summary] ⚫ 事件： 9 月中采制造业PMI 为 50.1%，较上月上行0.3 个百
分点，非制造业PMI 由 49.0%上行至50.2%。 
⚫ 制造业PMI 环比上行，中小企业景气改善。PMI 重回临界值
上方，生产回暖，价格上行为主要拉动。中、小型企业PMI
均上行，但仍处临近值下方，仅大型企业处于景气区间。 
⚫ 新旧动能分化逐步收敛，消费品行业重回景气区间，高技
术、装备制造仍具韧性。高技术、装备制造业高位小幅回
落，仍处于景气区间。基础原材料行业PMI 小幅上行，价格
上涨带来一定拉动。EPMI 大幅回暖，供需持续好转，采购
价格高位继续上行，就业边际回暖，但用户库存回落至荣枯
线下方，研发活动高位边际回落。消费品行业继续上行1.7
个百分点至50.7%。兰格钢铁PMI 受价格拉动作用较强，销
量、国内订单上行，库存水平下行至收缩区间。 
⚫ 生产保持旺盛，需求小幅回落，价格指标持续攀升。生产端
持续改善，进而推动企业采购需求。需求端来看，新订单、
新出口订单指数小幅下跌，但仍处景气区间，进口指数保持
上行。其中消费品制造业供需两旺，化工、黑色金属生产、
需求持续承压。价格指标持续高速上行，原材料和加工行业
剪刀差继续走扩，能化产业链为主要拉动项。原材料库存累
积，产成品库存去化，主动补库存周期尚不稳固。 
⚫ 服务业、建筑业PMI 重回景气区间。非制造业PMI 新订单指
数上行幅度最大，价格持续上行。其中，服务业受“双节”
效应带动，居民出行相关服务景气度回升。9 月财政资金落
地，“六张网”建设逐步显效，带动建筑业回暖。建筑业投
入品价格上行， 9 月钢材价格上行带动采购成本走高，而
销售价格回落，行业需求依然偏弱。 
⚫ 财政资金给四季度经济带来支撑，稳经济效果有待显现。9
月延续了成本拉动的特征，上下游企业利润分化格局仍将持
续。BCI 企业利润前瞻指数反弹，但成本压力短期难以减
轻，企业销售前瞻指数、融资环境指数小幅上行，未来仍需
关注价格传导效率。10 月受长假影响，PMI 环比或边际承
压。随着财政资金投放和逐步落地，基建的压舱石作用有望
显现， “六张网”建设进程提速，制造业投资在资金支持下
有望回暖。居民购房贴息政策在10 月落地，着力稳定房地
产市场，未来房地产投资的拖累作用有望边际减轻。  
⚫ 风险因素：地缘政治风险发展超预期，内需修复不及预期，
政策出台和落地不及预期。

万联证券研究所 www.wlzq.cn 第 3 页 共 7 页  
事件 
2026年9月，中采制造业PMI为50.1%， 较上月上行0.3个百分点， 非制造业PMI为50.2%，
较上月上行1.2个百分点，综合PMI为50.7%，环比上行1.2个百分点。

1    PMI 环比季节性回升，价格大幅走高 
制造业PMI环比季节性上行，中小企业景气改善。制造业PMI环比上行0.3个百分点至
50.1%，重回临界值上方，生产回暖，价格上行为主要拉动。从不同规模企业来看，
小型企业PMI上行1个百分点至48.9%，上行幅度相对最大，中型企业PMI上行0.3个百
分点至49.7%，大型企业PMI持平于上月的50.6%，仅大型企业处于景气区间，中小企
业依然承压。 
新旧动能分化逐步收敛，消费品行业重回景气区间，钢铁行业景气度上行，高技术、
装备制造高位边际回落，战略新兴产业显著回暖。高技术制造业继续下行0.4个百分
点至52.5%，装备制造业由51.4%回落为51%，高端制造景气度边际回落，但仍处景气
区间。基础原材料行业由47.9%上行至48%，价格带来一定拉动。消费品行业继续上行
1.7个百分点至50.7%，重回荣枯线上方。战略性新兴产业PMI（EPMI）由47.8%上行至
52.3%，其中，生产量、产品订货和采购量指数均大幅上行，出口订货指数持续上涨，
供需持续好转，采购价格高位继续上行，就业边际回暖，但用户库存回落至荣枯线下
方，研发活动高位边际回落。光伏经理人指数（SMI）8月中枢较7月回落，中上游制
造业下滑幅度较大，为主要拖累，下游电站小幅上行。兰格钢铁PMI由47.4%上行至
49.8%，其中，销售价格和采购成本大幅上行，销量、国内订单上行，出口订单回落，
库存水平下行至收缩区间。

图表1：9月制造业PMI环比季节性上行（%） 图表2：消费品行业PMI重回景气区间（%）

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

生产保持旺盛，需求小幅回落，出口 景气 小幅 降温 ， 但 外需 仍具 韧性， 企业 采购 意愿 
持续好转。分项来看，生产指数环比继续上涨1.3 个百分点至 51.7% ， 生产端持续改善 。 
需求端来看， 新订单指数环比转为下跌0.1个百分点至50.5%，供给好于需求的格局持
续，新出口订单指数环比也下跌0.1个百分点至50%，出口景气小幅回落，进口指数环

比继续上涨0.6个百分点至49.2%。生产旺盛推动企业采购需求，采购量指数上行0.5
个百分点至51%，供货商配送时间持平于上月的50.1%。从统计局公布数据来看，农副
食品加工、 医药等行业生产指数和新订单指数均高于55.0%，消费品制造业供需两旺；

图表3：新订单分项环比回落，价格大幅拉升（%） 图表4：美国制造业PMI攀升，海外景气持续（%）

价格指标持续上行，原材料和加工行业剪刀差继续走扩，能化产业链为主要拉动项。
主要原材料购进价格 指数上涨4.2个百分点至60.8%，持续高速上行，出厂价格上行
3.6个百分点至54%， 原材料价格涨幅高于出厂价格涨幅。从统计局公布数据来看，石
油煤炭及其他燃料加工、化学原料及化学制品等价格指数均升至60.0%以上高位，原
油价格持续上涨带动能化产品价格上行，也给企业加工制造带来一定抑制作用。 
原材料库存累积，产成品库存去化，主动补库存周期尚不稳固。产成品库存由48.4%
回落至47.6%， 原材料库存上行0.1个百分点至48.2%。原油带动能化产业链价格上行，
企业采购量回升，带动原材料库存上涨。企业产成品库存去化，显示企业当前依然依
靠消耗现有库存来应对增长。PMI生产经营活动预期指标持平于上月的53.8%。

2    非制造业 PMI 回暖，建筑业、服务业均上行 
非制造业景气水平明显改善，需求好转，价格上行。9月非制造业PMI为50.2%，较上
月上行1.2个百分点。其中，新订单指数上行幅度最大，但仍处荣枯线下方，新出口
订单指数上行，需求有所回升。商务活动重回景气区间，销售、投入品价格均上行至
景气区间。业务活动预期由55%上行至55.5%，企业经营预期改善。 
服务业PMI重回景气区间，新订单指数反弹，价格指数持续上行，“双节”效应带来一
定提振。服务业PMI为50.2%，较上月上行0.9个百分点。中秋和国庆长假有望带动居
民出行相关服务景气度回升。服务业新订单指数反弹上行，但仍处于临界值下方。服
务业投入品价格和销售价格均保持上行，业务活动预期与上月持平。从统计局公布数
据来看，电信广播电视及卫星传输服务、 货币金融服务、 保险等行业商务活动指数均
位于55.0%以上的高景气区间； 资本市场服务、 房地产等行业商务活动指数低位运行。 
建筑业PMI显著回暖，财政投放给基建带来支撑。建筑业PMI上行至50.3%，上月为
46.9%。其中，建筑业新订单指数上行3.3个百分点至45.7%，建筑业业务活动预期指
数上行3.6个百分点至55.4%，企业对近期市场发展信心好转。建筑业投入品价格上行， 
9月钢材价格上行带动采购成本走高，而销售价格回落，行业需求依然偏弱。9月财政
资金落地，“六张网”建设逐步显效，带动建筑业回暖。

图表7：建筑业PMI新订单、价格上行（%） 图表8：服务业新订单反弹上行(%)

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

制造业用工需求持续回落，服务业、建筑业就业好转。9月制造业PMI从业人员从上月
的48.7%继续下跌至48.4%。服务业从业人员较上月上行0.4个百分点至46.2%。建筑业
PMI从业人员指数44.3%，较上月继续上行1.3个百分点，随着建筑业走出施工淡季，
用工需求逐步好转。

3    总结与展望 
财政资金给四季度经济带来支撑，稳经济效果有待显现。9月延续了成本拉动的特征，
上下游企业利润分化格局仍将持续。BCI企业利润前瞻指数反弹，但成本压力短期难
以减轻，企业销售前瞻指数、融资环境指数小幅上行，未来仍需关注价格传导效率。
10月受长假影响，PMI环比或边际承压。随着财政资金投放和逐步落地，基建的压舱
石作用有望显现， “六张网”建设进程提速，制造业投资在资金支持下有望回暖。居
民购房贴息政策在10月落地，着力稳定房地产市场，未来房地产投资的拖累作用有望
边际减轻。

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

## Document 146: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-09-17
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

00

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 
기다렸던 4 행정 중속 엔진 캐파 증설 단행 
HD현대중공업의 4행정 엔진 캐파는 기존 3.0GW에서 증설이 완료되는 '28년부터 
램프업을 거쳐 '30년 7.2GW까지 확대될 계획이다. ①[육상 발전용]은 신공장(울산 
온산) 3GW 와 HD 현대엔진(전남 영암) 1GW 를 합쳐 4.0GW, ②[선박용]은 
2.3GW에서 3.2GW로 확대된다. HD현대엔진 캐파는 기존 0.7GW에서 1GW까
지 확대되며, 해당 라인은 신공장이 가동되기 전 ‘27~’28 년 구간에서의 선박&육상 엔
진 모두 대응 가능하다. 신공장 캐파는 총 3.0GW로, ‘28년 약 30%의 가동률로 시
작해 램프업을 거쳐 ‘30 년부터 풀캐파로 20MW급 모델을 생산할 예정이다. 4 행정 
중속엔진에 대한 수요 가시성은 높다. 동사가 공유했던 육상 발전 엔진 인콰이어리 규
모는 기존 약 3~9GW였으나, 현재는 이를 크게 상회하는 수준으로 소통하고 있다.

최근 부유식 데이터센터(FDC)에 대한 하이퍼스케일러들의 인콰이어리 또한 확대되고 
있다. HD 한국조선해양은 최근 미국 선급(ABS)로부터 100MW급 규모의 부유식 발
전 설비(BMPP)에 대한 기본 인증(AiP)를 획득했으며, 동사는 FDC 신조 사업과 더불
어 발전원 4 행정 엔진을 파워십 및 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)에 납품하며 그룹사들과 
패키지 사업으로 진행할 예정이다. 가시화된 수요를 기반으로 엔진기계사업부의 구조적 
성장 보여줄 것으로 전망하며, 이에 따른 리레이팅을 예상한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 및 목표주가 기존 97 만원 유지 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’ 및 목표주가 기존 97 만원을 유지한다. 동사는 
올해 상선수주 목표 이미 초과달성하며 안정적인 펀더멘털 유지 중이다. 하반기 남은 
기간은 LNGC, VLGC/VLAC 중심의 선별 수주 전략을 통해 ‘28 년 이후의 상선 부
문 수익성 유지 충분히 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 글로벌 지정학적 리스크 확대에 따른 
해군 현대화 사이클 기조에 맞추어 미 해군은 동맹국의 호위함 구매를 고려하고 있으
며, MASGA를 기반으로 중장기적으로 미국 전투함을 건조하기 위해 HD현대의 미국 
진출 가능성 높다는 판단이다. 또한 미국을 제외한 글로벌 함정(페루, 필리핀, 말레이시
아, 칠레, 사우디, 이집트 등) 수주 파이프라인도 다수 보유하고 있어 함정 모멘텀에 따
른 리레이팅 충분히 가능할 전망이다. 특수선+4 행정 엔진+FDC+SMR 등 조선소에 
붙일 수 있는 모든 추가 성장동력을 보유한 유일한 종목이기에 조선업 최선호주(Top-
Pick)로 제시한다. 최근 불거지고 있는 노조 파업 문제 해소될 시 저점 매수 기회라는 
판단이다.

报告要点  
中国宏观经济的运行底色正在发生一次范式级别的迁移：从以地产和土地财政为锚的
周期性波动，切换为由财政收支结构转型驱动的 "结构性调整"。我们认为，2025 年作
为"十四五"收官与"十五五"开局的关键衔接点，财政在收入端、债务端、支出端三个维
度加速方向性的"出清—约束—重置"，其本质是一次宏观信用体系的重置—— 旧的信
用扩张锚在资产价格与债务约束下触及上限，新的信用创造锚（中央财政＋国家信用
＋科技新基建）正在通过赤字、超长期特别国债与产业政策体系加速确立。这一切换
不是短期逆周期操作的量级变化， 而是决定未来五年乃至更长周期中国经济潜在增速、
价格中枢与资产定价基准的结构性变量。 
收入端正在经历传统地租经济的 "L 型"筑底， 且收缩正在向"第一财政"传导。政府性基
金收入增速自 2022 年起连续四年负增长，但斜率出现边际放缓； 全国一般公共预算收
入在 2025 年录得-1.7%的负增长，但结构上需区分看待：多数省份税收增速已恢复正
增长， 整体负增长主要由非税收入的回落所致。 国有土地使用权出让收入2025 年降至
4.15 万亿元、同比-14.7%，较 2021 年峰值已腰斩。我们认为，这一收缩是底层资产
价格预期重构后的"去资本化"，地方财政的"紧平衡"将常态化而非周期性。 
债务端则形成了存量博弈下的防风险挤占。地方政府债务余额在 2025 年继续攀升， 付
息额同步扩张， 利息支出率在青海、 甘肃、 天津等省份持续攀升。 宽口径负债率 （含城
投有息债务） 在浙江、 江苏等经济大省全面上行， 在甘肃等尾部省份处于绝对高位。 我
们认为，债务率的空间锁定与城投信用派生能力的停滞，正迫使地方政府系统性退出
宏观固定资产投资扩张的第一线，"化债—财政缺口—增长"的三角矛盾进入 最深的存
量博弈阶段。 
支出端则开启事权上收与中央扩表的全面重置。 2025 年赤字率历史性突破 4%，2026
年"十五五"开局维持约 4%的赤字率、5.89 万亿元赤字规模， 超长期特别国债发行规模
达 1.3 万亿元，中央对地方转移支付升至 10.4 万亿元量级。一个极具信号意义的变化
是：政府性基金支出增速在 2025 年大幅回升至+11.26% ，广义财政支出首次突破 40
万亿元——化债资金与特别国债开始落地，中央信用正在对冲地方收缩。我们认为，
财政资源的配置重心正从"铺摊子的铁公基"硬性切换到"补能力的新科技"：2025 年研
发经费投入强度升至 2.80%、首次超过 OECD 平均水平，高技术制造业增加值占规上
工业比重升至 17.1%，全员劳动生产率增速（6.1%）重新跑赢 GDP 增速（5.0%） 
更值得关注的是，上述变化并非财政收入、债务和支出三个维度的孤立调整，而是共
同指向宏观信用创造机制的重构。这一财政生态的重构，将直接 重塑资本市场的资产
定价范式。 固收端，无风险利率中枢的长周期下移与城投债信用利差的趋势性走阔将
并行演绎， 主权信用与地方信用的"二元分化"正式确立； 权益端， 传统依赖政府订单与
土地链条的 To-G 端资产面临估值体系的系统性下修，而由主权信用背书、承接"新质
生产力"资本开支的科技硬资产与算力基础设施，将获得跨越周期的风险溢价重估。我
们认为，"十五五"期间资本市场最核心的配置命题， 是在财政信用重置的背景下， 完成
从"土地信用资产"向"国家信用资产"的定价切换

### Page 2

请仔细阅读本报告末页声明                                                                                  Page 2 / 19

[Table_Page]  宏观研究  
2026 年 9 月 24 日

风险提示：  1、若房地产与土地市场下行幅度超预期，政府性基金收入修复迟于预期，地方财政紧平衡或进一步加剧，化债进度与稳增长诉求
之间的平衡难度上升； 
2、财政扩张若仍以结构性投放为主、总量托底力度不足，或难以有效对冲需求缺口，价格中枢低位运行的时间可能拉长； 
3、科技与算力资本开支若未能及时形成可计量的生产率回报，中央加杠杆的持续性及其对私人部门的挤出效应存在不确定性。

[Table_Page]  宏观研究  
2026 年 9 月 24 日

一、 宏观总揽与"十五五"前瞻：宏观信用重置的范式迁移 
（一） 经济波动底色的变迁：从周期性下行到发展模式的 "结构性转型" 
过去四十余年中国宏观经济的波动， 本质上是由"信用扩张—资产价格上涨 —财政增收 —再投
资"这一链条驱动的周期性波动。 在土地与人口红利尚存、 地方政府债务约束相对宽松的阶 段，
经济下行通常只是库存周期与信用周期的短暂回落， 政策通过放松地产与基建即可实 现 V 型
复苏。2008 年金融危机后"四万亿"刺激之所以能够在短期实现经济增速的快速回升，其底层
机制正是以土地作为抵押品、以地方平台作为融资主体的信用扩张循环仍然畅通。  
但 2020 年以来的这一轮下行，性质已经发生根本改变。我们认为，当前经济面临的 并非单
纯的周期性回落， 而是发展模式的"结构性转型 "——支撑上一轮信用扩张的底层资产 （ 土地）
价格预期出现不可逆的重构，使得" 土地—城投—基建"这一信用派生机制触及了模式的 天然
上限。观察财政数据可以清晰地看到这一底色切换的深化：政府性基金收入自 2022 年起连
续四年负增长，而全国一般公共预算收入在 2025 年录得-1.7% 的负增长（21.6 万亿元 ） ，为
2020 年疫情之外的首个负增长年份。 不过结构上需区分： 这一负增长主要由非税收入 的回落
所致，税收收入本身在多数省份已恢复正增长 ——这意味着土地财政的 偏弱目前仍主 要停留
在"第二财政"（政府性基金） 层面， 尚未全面击穿税收这一"第一财政"的底盘。 价格层面，20 23
年以来 CPI 多个月份负增长、PPI 长期处于收缩区间， "高债务—低通胀—低增长" 的 组合，
正是信用扩张旧机制触及上限、新机制尚未完全接续这一转轨期的典型特征。  
图表 1：GDP与财政"两本账"收支增速（2018—2025,%）

资料来源：choice 、if ind, 五矿证券研究所整理 
上图清晰地呈现了这一底色切换：GDP 增速在 2022 年之后维持在 5%附近的"合理 区间" ，
而政府性基金收入增速则在 2022 年深度跌入负区间后持续徘徊。 两个细节值得强调： 其一，
政府性基金收入增速在 2025 年收窄至 -7.04%， 连续第四年负增长但斜率放缓， 土地 财政" 最
陡峭的收缩段"可能已经过去；其二，一般公共预算收入增速在 2025 年跌至-1.68%、跌破零
轴， 但需拆解其结构——这一负增长主要由非税收入回落所致 （2024 年罚没等一次性 收入基
数偏高） ，而税收收入在多数省份已恢复正增长，说明财政"基本盘"的税收底盘尚未松 动，真
正的压力仍在政府性基金与土地财政一侧。 
从"十五五"规划的顶层设计看， 政策层面对这一结构性转型的认知已相当清晰。 《中华 人民共
和国国民经济和社会发展第十五个五年规划纲要》将 2030 年阶段性目标明确为 "质的 有效提
升＋量的合理增长"， 并设定劳动生产率增速高于GDP 增速、 研发经费投入年均增速高 于7%
等指向效率的约束性目标 （见下表） 。 这意味着， 未来五年宏观政策的核心任务，不再 是维持
旧的信用扩张循环，而是完成信用创造机制的系统性重置 ——这正是我们判断 "十五 五" 是中
国经济范式切换期的根本依据。

需要强调的是，这一结构性转型并非无成本。旧的信用扩张机制虽然在效率上已经衰 竭，但
在存量上仍是维持地方运转与债务滚动的基础；新机制的接续需要时间，且伴随着地 方财政
的"缩表阵痛"与资产定价的系统性重估。理解这一点，是理解 "十五五"期间宏观政策 " 既 要防
风险、又要促转型"两难处境的钥匙 
（二） 信用扩张旧锚的触及上限： "地方政府+土地财政+城投平台"的扩张模
式受到多重制约  
"地方政府＋土地财政＋城投平台"三位一体，构成了过去二十年中国宏观信用扩张的旧 锚。
其运行逻辑是一个闭环：地方政府以土地出让收入和土地抵押作为信用基础，通 过城 投平台
举债投资基础设施，推升地价与资产价格，进而反哺财政与债务循环。这一模式在土 地价格
单边上涨、人口城镇化快速推进的阶段高度自洽，也因此在 2018—2021 年间被推至 极致 —
—部分东部省份财政对土地出让金的依赖度一度超过 40%并逼近 50%。 
然而旧锚的失效正在于其内在的脆弱性。我们将其归结为三重约束的同时收紧：  
其一，资产价格预期的重构打破了循环的自我强化。 土地作为一种"资本化"资产，其价值取
决于对未来租金现金流与增值预期的贴现。当人口结构逆转、城镇化斜率放缓、居民 部门资
产负债表收缩三重因素叠加， 土地的长期增值预期被根本性打破，"举债—投资—抬升 地价 —
再举债"的正反馈随之逆转， 地方财政的抵押品价值缩水， 优质可融资资产日益稀缺。2 02 5年
国有土地使用权出让收入降至 4.15 万亿元、 同比-14.7%，较 2021 年峰值已腰斩，正 是这一
"去资本化"的直接体现。 
其二，债务约束的制度性收紧封堵了信用派生的旁路。 自 2023 年"一揽子化债方案"以来， 政
策通过增加债务限额置换存量隐性债务、严格管控新增隐性债务、推动融资平台 "退 平台" 转
型，城投平台的准财政融资职能被系统性剥离。 2024 年 11 月推出的 12 万亿元化债 " 组合拳
"（6 万亿元置换额度分三年实施＋连续五年每年 8000 亿元专项债置换＋ 2 万亿元棚改 隐债 ） ，
标志着"企业化财政"这一延续二十年的准财政机制正在走向制度性终结。  
其三， 投资的边际产出效率已降至极低水平。 我们测算，2024 年中国每实现 1 元名义 GDP，
需投入约 6.0 元信贷资金，处于除 2020 年以外的历史高位。增量资本产出率（ ICO R）的持
续恶化意味着，旧模式下信用扩张对增长的拉动已接近极限， "债务增、回报降 " 的脱节 局面
日益严峻。 
我们认为，旧模式在多重约束下已难以持续，其信用派生能力显著减弱 。这并非政策 层的主
观选择，而是模式自身的经济学边界

2026 年 9 月 24 日

（三） 信用创造新锚的确立： "中央财政+国家信用+科技新基建"开启全要素
生产率新周期  
与旧锚的触及上限相对应，新的信用创造锚正在加速确立，其主体结构、信用载体与 投资方
向均发生了根本性迁移。我们用三个维度的切换来刻画这一迁移：  
从主体结构看，信用扩张的主体从 "地方 "切换到 "中央 "。2025 年赤字率历史性突破 4%、赤
字规模提升至 5.66 万亿元，2026 年"十五五"开局进一步将赤字规模抬升至 5.89 万亿元（赤
字率约 4%） ， 且增量全部列于中央本级。 以2026 年预算报告为基准（见下表） ，中央 财政已
成为四本账中信用扩张与资本形成的唯一主渠道， 而地方财政的功能定位则系统性转 向" 公共
服务保障与债务风险处置"。 这是对"中央加杠杆、 地方防风险"这一新信用分工的制度化确 认。 
图表 3：《2026 年预算报告》归纳 
预算账户  2026 年规模 结构性要点 
一般公共预算 收入 22.07 万亿／支出 30.01 万亿 
税收 18.15 万亿、非税 3.92 万亿；赤字5.89
万亿补缺口 
政府性基金预算 本级收入 5.81 万亿／支出 11.87 万亿 特别国债 1.3 万亿、专项债 4.4 万亿 
国有资本经营预算 收入 0.80 万亿／支出 0.29 万亿 调入一般公共预算 0.53 万亿 
社会保险基金预算 收入 13.09 万亿／支出 11.81 万亿 保费 9.39 万亿、财政补贴 3.11 万亿 
资料来源：财政部《2026 年预算 报告》, 五矿证券研究所整理 
从信用载体看，信用扩张的抵押逻辑从 "土地"切换到 "国家信用 "。过去地方政府以土地作为
抵押品撬动债务，如今中央财政以主权信用直接融资，投向的资产从可变现的地产项 目转向
承载国家战略的科技与基础设施，其偿债逻辑从"土地增值"切换为"生产率提升带来的 广义税
基扩大"。超长期特别国债发行规模达 1.3 万亿元，成为中央表内加杠杆的主渠道， 其 " 超长
期"的期限特征，正对应着新周期长回报周期的资本开支属性。这一切换使得债务的 性质从 "
被动滚动"转向"主动的资本形成"，是理解新锚的关键差异。  
从投资方向看， 信用扩张的投向从 "铁公基 "切换到"科技新基建 "。2025 年的关键指标已经清
晰显现这一切换（见下表） ：研发经费投入强度升至2.80%、首次超过 OECD 平均水 平，研
发经费支出达 3.93 万亿元、同比+8.1%；高技术制造业增加值占规上工业比重升至 17.1% 、
增加值同比+9.4%；装备制造业占规上工业比重升至 36.8%；工业机器人产量达 77.3 万 套、
同比+28.0%；全员劳动生产率增速（+6.1% ）重新跑赢 GDP 增速（5.0%）

图表 5：实体经济部门与非金融企业部门杠杆率（%, 2001-2025）

资料来源：if ind, 五矿证券研究所整理 
从杠杆结构看，实体经济部门杠杆率在 2025 年三季度突破 300%（约 302%） 、非金 融企业
部门升至约 174%， 但在"中央加杠杆、地方稳杠杆、 居民与企业去杠杆"的新结构下，杠杆的
内涵正在从"土地抵押的无效扩张 " 转向" 国家信用背书的生产率投资 " ，这是新旧周 期最 本质
的差异。 
我们认为， 新旧锚的切换， 实质是宏观信用从"土地增值驱动 "向"全要素生产率驱动 " 的 范式迁
移。如果说旧锚对应的是外延式、重资产、高杠杆的增长模式，新锚对应的则是内涵 式、重
效率、重创新的增长模式。这一迁移的完成速度与质量，将直接决定 "十五五"期间中国 经济
的潜在增速中枢与资产定价基准， " 十五五" 规划的 2030 年目标 —— 劳动生产率增 速 高于
GDP 增速、研发投入年均>7%、数字核心产业增加值占比提升至 12.5%——正是新 锚确立
路径的量化表达。 
二、 收入端出清： 传统地租经济的"L 型"筑底与地方紧平衡常态化 
（一） 土地财政依赖度的长周期下移：底层资产价格预期重构与去资本化  
土地财政依赖度的下移，是收入端结构性转型最核心的信号。自 1998 年房改以来， 地方财
政对土地出让金的依赖持续扩大，这一依赖在 2018—2021 年达到顶峰，部分省份财 政对土
地出让金的依赖度一度超过 40%。但 2021 年以来，房地产市场的深度调整使得土地 出让这
一"第二财政"经历了剧烈的去资本化过程。 
从全国口径看， 国有土地使用权出让收入在2025 年降至 4.15 万亿元、 同比-14.7%，较20 21
年约 8.5 万亿元的峰值已腰斩。从分省数据观察 （见下表） ，收缩的普遍性与深度远超 一般意
义上的周期回落：广东政府性基金收入从 2021 年的约 8491 亿元降至 2024 年的约 3 45 2亿
元、累计收缩约 60%，江苏从 2021 年的约 1.36 万亿元降至 2024 年的约 8097 亿元 、累计
收缩约 41%， 浙江累计收缩约47%。 经济大省尚且如此， 中西部财政薄弱省份的收缩 更为剧
烈，土地财政对地方财力的支撑能力出现了系统性、不可逆的下移。  
图表 6：政府性基金收入分省数据（亿元；2018-2024） 
省份  2018 2020 2021 2022 2023 2024 
广东  5,887 8,642 8,491 5,196 4,502 3,452 
江苏  8,223 11,359 13,633 11,479 10,316 8,097 
浙江  8,737 11,353 11,647 10,069 8,363 6,169 
山东  6,001 7,279 7,977 6,080 4,925 4,832 
资料来源：Choice、ifind、Wind，五矿 证券研 究所整理（注 ：分省 数据更 新至2024，全国 口径2025 年土地 出让金4.15 万亿元 ）

### Page 8

请仔细阅读本报告末页声明                                                                                  Page 8 / 19

[Table_Page]  宏观研究  
2026 年 9 月 24 日

我们认为，这一收缩的实质是底层资产价格预期的重构。土地作为一种 "资本化" 资产， 其价
值取决于对未来租金现金流与增值预期的贴现。当人口结构逆转、城镇化斜率放缓、 居民部
门资产负债表收缩三重因素叠加， 土地的长期增值预期被根本性打破， 土地财政便从" 增量扩
张"的引擎蜕变为"存量收缩"的拖累。这一"去资本化 "的过程，决定了土地财政的修复不 是简
单的 V 型反弹，而是长周期的"L 型"筑底——这也是我们认 为地方财政紧平衡将 "常 态化" 而
非"周期性"的根本原因。从数据上看，2025 年政府性基金收入仍为负增长 ，但降幅已 经明显
收窄，这种“水平继续下降、下降斜率开始收敛 ”的组合，正是由快速下台阶向低位稳态 过渡的
早期特征。 
这里所称的“L 型筑底”，并不是指土地财政已经见底 ，也不是假设其未来不再下降 ，而 是指经
过前期一次性的资产价格重估和信用收缩之后，其下降斜率将逐步收敛，并在明显低 于过去
的平台上形成新的稳态。 这个稳态具有现实基础： 一方面， 城镇化由高速扩张转向低速 推进，
并不意味着土地需求消失，人口继续向都市圈和核心城市集中，城市更新、旧城改造 以及产
业空间重构仍会形成持续的土地再配置需求；另一方面，土地供给本身具有较强的行 政调节
属性，当需求和价格下降后，地方政府会相应压缩供地规模 ，使土地收入难以长期按 照此前
相同的斜率持续收缩。房地产由“增量开发 ”转向“存量更新 ”之后，土地财政的角色也将 从信用
扩张的发动机退化为存量资产再配置所形成的经常性收入，其规模中枢显著下移，但 并不会
归零。 
需要进一步强调的是"去资本化" 与普通周期下行的本质区别：普通下行中，资产价格回 落但
估值逻辑不变，价格跌到一定程度会有买盘承接、企稳回升；而 "去资本化" 意味着支撑 资产
估值的长周期变量（人口、城镇化斜率、杠杆空间）已经逆转，价格中枢的系统性下 移不会
因为短期的政策放松而逆转。这正是土地财政"L 型筑底"而非"V 型反弹"的深层逻辑，也是收
入端出清的第一重、 也是最底层的约束。 不过，2025 年政府性基金收入降幅收窄至 -7. 04%，
也提示"最陡峭的收缩段"可能正在过去，收入端出清正从 "急跌"转入"缓跌"，这一斜率 变化值
得跟踪。 
（二） 政府性基金收入连续负增长：从增量扩张转向存量博弈  
政府性基金收入是土地财政的镜像。从全国 "四本账"数据观察，政府性基金预算收入的 增速
自 2022 年起连续四年深度负增长：2021 年尚为+4.85% ，2022 年骤降至 -20.55%，2 02 3年
为-9.21%，2024 年为-12.18%，2025 年收窄至-7.04%（见图表 1） 。全国政府性基金 预算收
入降至约 5.77 万亿元。四年累计的收缩 ，使政府性基金预算从财政的 "第二增长极 " 蜕变为 "
最大的收缩来源"。 
更值得关注的是结构性的错位与 2025 年的反转信号。 2022—2024 年，tu 端由于在 建项目
续建、 保障房配套等刚性需求， 收缩幅度远小于收入端， 导致收支缺口持续扩大。 但2 02 5年
政府性基金支出增速大幅回升至+11.26%，与收入端的 -7.04%形成明显的反向剪刀差 。这一
反转的核心驱动，是化债置换资金与超长期特别国债的落地 ——中央信用开始大规模 注入政
府性基金， 对冲地方土地收入的收缩。2025 年广义财政支出 （一般公共预算支出＋政 府性基
金支出）首次突破 40 万亿元，正是这一对冲的总量体现。  
专项债在这一背景下经历了功能的重塑。在 2022—2024 年， 专项债逐步演化为"缺口 填补工
具"——从本应服务于有收益项目的融资工具， 退化为弥补土地出让收入回落后地方投 资资金
来源的准预算平衡工具，在山西、江西、贵州、甘肃、青海、云南等中西部财政薄弱 地区尤
为突出。2025 年起，随着化债专项债的放量发行，专项债进一步承担起 "置换存量隐 性债务 "
的职能，其"融资抓手"属性加速让位于"保运转、化存量 "属性。这种" 缺口靠债补，债务 靠再
融资"的路径依赖， 一方面背离了专项债与财政预算表外安排相对独立的初衷， 另一方 面也模
糊了债务责任边界。

[Table_Page]  宏观研究  
2026 年 9 月 24 日

这一"从增量扩张转向存量博弈" 的转变，意味着地方财政的可用资金池在长周期内系统 性收
缩。 新发债券中相当比重被用于"借新还旧"与"债务置换"， 真正流向新增投资与资本形 成的资
金比重持续下降，"还旧不生新"的格局使得财政对经济增长的边际拉动明显减弱。 我们 认为，
这不仅是收入端的出清，更是地方财政 "扩表"能力的历史性退潮 ——专项债从 "撬动增 长 " 的
工具退化为"维持运转"的工具，是收入端出清最直接的制度性表征。  
（三） 地方财政的 "紧平衡"常态化：财政长周期"缩表"与宏观乘数效应的衰
减 
收入端出清的最终落点， 是地方财政" 紧平衡"的常态化， 其最直观的表征是"第一财政 "（一般
公共预算）的失守与地方财政自给率、税收占比的结构性走弱。  
最需要关注的新信号， 是一般公共预算收入的转负及其结构。2025 年全国一般公共预 算收入
录得 21.6 万亿元、同比-1.7%，为 2020 年疫情之外的首个负增长年份。但拆开看， 这一负
增长并非"税收走弱"，而是"税收修复、非税回落 " ：多数省份税收增速已恢复正增 长（ 广东
+4.0%、江苏+3.8%、上海+3.3%、山东+1.4% ） ，拖累整体的是非税收入的大幅回落（20 24
年罚没等一次性收入基数偏高，2025 年"挤水分" ） 。这一结构的宏观含义在于：土地 财政的
偏弱目前仍主要停留在政府性基金这一 "第二财政 "，尚未全面击穿税收这一 " 第一财政 " 的底
盘；但一般公共预算支出的近零增长（+0.1% ）与刚性民生支出的持续扩张，仍意味着 地方
财政的紧平衡在延续。 
从地方财政自给率（一般公共预算收入/一般公共预算支出）看， 2024 年（注：自给 率分省
数据目前更新至 2024）全国形成了鲜明的两极分化格局（见下表） ：上海（ 84.8% ） 、 北京
（75.9%） 、广东（75.4%） 、浙江（69.3%） 、江苏（65.6%）等东部经济大省凭借广税 基维
持着较强的财政自主性； 而西藏 （9.5% ） 、 甘肃 （22.0%） 、黑龙江（22.5%） 、青海 （17.1% ）
等西部与东北省份则长期处于高度依赖转移支付的低自给状态。更需警惕的是，即便 东部省
份的自给率也较 2018年普遍回落——上海从 85.1%降至 84.8%， 广东从77.0%降至 75.4% ，
浙江从 76.5%降至 69.3%——反映出土地财政收缩对全域财政的传导。

从税收占比（税收收入/一般公共预算收入）看，这一反映财政收入 "含金量"的指标同 样在走
弱。广东从 2018 年的 80.4%降至 2024 年的 72.5%，山东从 75.5%降至 65.4% ， 江苏从
84.2%降至 76.1%。税收占比的下移，一方面源于减税降费的持续累积效应，另一方 面更源
于土地相关税费与房地产链条税收的萎缩，非税收入的被动扩张在一定程度上填补 了缺 口，
但也稀释了财政收入的可持续性。

我们认为，"低弹性收入＋高刚性支出"的组合， 构成了地方财政的长期"缩表"。 值得注意 的是，
2025 年一般公共预算支出增速仅+0.1% ， 几乎零增长， 而社会保障和就业支出仍保持+6.7%
的增长——刚性支出的扩张与收入端的收缩形成剪刀差，压缩了地方财政的自主调 节空 间，
也使得财政支出的宏观乘数效应系统性衰减。从支出项目看，教育、社会保障和就业 、医疗
卫生等刚性支出持续正增长，而一般公共服务、文化体育、交通运输等弹性支出则成 为压缩
的主要对象——这种"保刚性、 压弹性"的结构，正是财政"缩表"时期典型的支出行为特 征。这
意味着，地方财政面临的核心问题已经从“如何扩大财政支出”逐步转向“如何在 有 限财力
下重新配置支出” 。 当财政收入无法覆盖原有投资和支出需求时， 过去通过地方政府和 融资平
台加杠杆弥补资金缺口的方式也受到约束。因此，收入端的紧平衡最终需要通过地方 资产负
债表进行消化，并进一步表现为地方信用扩张能力的下降  
在"新质生产力"尚未形成稳定的财政回报、无法有效反哺地方财力之前，地方财政的紧 平衡
将是一个需要以"五年"甚至更长尺度来观察的结构性状态，而非短期可逆的周期现象。 这一
判断，是我们对"十五五"期间地方财政行为进行前瞻的基准假设 ——地方政府的功能定 位将
更加聚焦于公共服务保障与债务风险处置，其在宏 观投资扩张中的角色将逐步调整  
三、 债务端约束：存量博弈下的防风险挤占效应与地方扩张退出 
（一） 宏观 杠杆的空间锁定：经济大省与尾部省份宽口径负债率的全面攀升 
债务端的核心变化，是宏观杠杆的空间被系统性锁定。地方政府债务余额在 2025 年 继续攀
升（见下表） ：江苏从2015 年的约 1.06 万亿元升至 2025 年的约 3.39 万亿元，广 东从约
8188 亿元升至约 3.70 万亿元，山东从约 9059 亿元升至约 3.33 万亿元，四川从约 74 65亿
元升至约 2.80 万亿元。全国地方政府债务余额总量已逼近 50 万亿元量级。显性债务 的扩张
尚且在限额管理的框架内， 若将城投平台有息债务纳入宽口径考量，负债率的攀升更为显 著 。

其债务扩张的空间也在快速收窄 ——这并非因为经济大省 "缺钱"，而是因为" 以债养债 " 的模
式触及了效率与安全的双重边界。

与此同时，尾部省份的宽口径负债率处于绝对高位。甘肃的宽口径负债率在 2025 年 达到约
141%，青海约 98%，宁夏约 74%。这些省份的债务存量相对其经济体量与综合财力 而言已
严重失衡，偿债能力的薄弱叠加债务存量的高企，构成了潜在的尾部风险。需要说 明的 是，
宽口径负债率的绝对水平较我们此前基于旧版城投有息债务数据的测算有所下修（旧 版尾部
省份曾达 2—4 倍） ，但"经济大省持续攀升、尾部省份绝对高位 "的结构性特征并未改 变，区
域间的债务风险分层反而更加清晰。 
我们认为，"经济大省负债率攀升、 尾部省份负债率绝对高位 "的双重锁定，是"十五五" 期间地
方财政扩张退出的结构性背景。宏观杠杆已难以再通过地方加杠杆的方式继续抬升 —— 经济
大省加杠杆空间收窄，尾部省份加杠杆能力丧失，地方财政的 "扩表"通道被系统性关闭 。这
是债务端约束的第一重逻辑。 
（二） 利息支出率的深度挤占：化债背景下地方政府被迫退出宏观固投扩张
第一线

的绝对量观察（见下表） ，2025 年继续扩张：广东突破 1000 亿元（1004.6 亿元） ， 山东达
898.5 亿元，江苏达 868.5 亿元，四川达 768.5 亿元，湖南达 664.5 亿元。付息额的 持续增

湖南  20.1 430.3 608.2 664.5 33 倍 
青海  10.5 88.2 110.1 112.5 11 倍 
宁夏  6.4 68.0 75.6 78.3 12 倍 
资料来源：if ind, 五矿证券研究所整理 
付息额的扩张速度远超同期财政收入的增长速度， 这是理解"付息挤占"的关键。 以广东 为例，
付息额从 2015 年的 17.0 亿元增至 2025 年的 1004.6 亿元、增长 59 倍，而同期广东 一般公
共预算收入仅增长约四成；山东付息额增长 53 倍，江苏增长 38 倍，四川增长 31 倍 。付息
额增速与收入增速的巨大反差，意味着利息支出在地方可用财力中的占比被急剧抬高 —— 这
正是"存量债务刚性付息挤占新增投资 "的微观机制。从债务结构看，专项债付息已成为 付息
额的主体，且其增速高于一般债付息，反映出近年来专项债高速扩张所积累的付息压 力正在
集中释放。换言之，即便化债置换压低了平均融资利率，付息额的绝对量仍因债务存 量的扩
张而持续攀升，利息负担的"总量刚性"并未因"利率下行"而缓解。 
以利息支出率（地方政府债券付息额/ （一般预算收入＋政府性基金收入） ）衡量，尾部 省份
的挤占效应在 2025 年进一步加剧（见图表 12） ：青海升至约27.3%，甘肃升至约 17.7% ，
天津升至约 13.9%，宁夏约 12.1%，江苏约 5.2%。这意味着，在 土地财政收缩与一 般公共
预算收入转负的叠加下，相当比例的地方可用财力被用于偿还债务利息，真正可用于 投资与
公共服务的资金被系统性挤占。 
图表 12：利息支出率（债券付息/两本账收入, 2015—2025, %）  图表 13：地方赤字率（两本账收支缺口/GDP, 2015—2025, %）

资料来源：if ind, 五矿证券研究所整理  资料来源：if ind, 五矿证券研究所整理 
我们认为， 利息支出率的深度挤占， 是地方财政"退出宏观固投扩张第一线 "的直接财务 动因。
当付息成为地方财政的第一优先项时，投资扩张的空间便被压缩到极限 ——这正是化 债背景
下"防风险挤占稳增长"的微观机制。从地方赤字率（两本账收支缺口 /GDP）看，经济 大省维
持在温和水平，而甘肃、青海等尾部省份仍处于较高的结构性失衡状态，进一步印证 了地方
财政扩张能力的区域性坍塌。 
我们此前测算，本轮债务付息率（占 GDP）在 2027 年前后才有望与债务率出现交 叉 、进入
实质性压降区间。在此之前，地方财政的付息压力将持续构成对投资扩张的刚性约束 。综合
债务端的三重逻辑：地方政府债务余额持续上升、城投平台信用派 生能力受到约束以 及利息
支出对可用财力的挤占，共同构成地方财政继续大规模扩张投资的约束条件。在债务 存量得

### Page 13

请仔细阅读本报告末页声明                                                                                  Page 13 / 19

[Table_Page]  宏观研究  
2026 年 9 月 24 日

到实质性缓释之前，地方财政更需要优先完成债务风险处置和基本公共服务保障，其 在宏观
固定资产投资中的角色将由过去的主要扩张者逐步转向项目执行与风险管理者。这并 不意味
着地方投资在“十五五”期间完全退出，而是意味着新增宏观信用和战略性资本形成 需要更
多依靠中央财政及其他市场化主体进行承接。由此，债务端的约束自然引出支出端的 一个核
心变化：当地方信用扩张空间收窄之后，中央财政如何承担更多总量稳定职能，以及 新增财
政信用最终投向何处，将成为下一阶段宏观政策和经济增长模式变化的关键。  
四、 支出端重置：事权上收、中央扩表与科技固投化的全面崛起 
（一） 利息支出率的深度挤占：化债背景下地方政府被迫退出宏观固投扩张
第一线  
收入端与债务端的双重出清，倒逼支出端的结构性重置，而这一重置的制度基础，是 财税体
制的深层改革。 二十届三中全会明确提出"建立权责清晰、 财力协调、 区域均衡的中央 和地方
财政关系"，并首次系统性提出" 增加地方自主财力，拓展地方税源，适当扩大地方税收 管理
权限"。这一表述的背后，是对"地方事权过大、财权不足"这一结构性失衡的制度性纠 偏 ——
过去地方政府承担了过重的公共服务与建设职能，却缺乏稳定、可持续的制度化收 入来 源，
只能依赖土地财政这一不稳定、非规范的渠道。  
"十五五"期间，事权与财权的上移正在加速，具体体现在三个层面：  
其一，中央本级支出与中央事权比重适当提高。 2026 年中央本级支出约 4.54 万亿元、同比
+5.5%，中央在国防、科技、跨区域重大基础设施等领域的事权与支出责任系统性增强。  
其二，转移支付成为平衡区域财力的主渠道。 中央对地方转移支付规模升至 10.4 万亿元量
级， 通过"财力下沉"兜底基层"三保"， 缓解区域间财政能力的结构性分化。转移支付的 功能定
位也从"弥补缺口"升级为"绩效导向的财力再分配"。 
其三，地方税体系的培育迈出实质性步伐。 消费税征收环节后移并稳步下划地方，被视为地
方税体系改革的关键一步，旨在为地方财政培育一个与消费挂钩的稳定税源，逐步替 代对土
地财政的依赖。同时，提高直接税比重、完善个税综合征收、健全财产所得税收政策 等改革
方向，共同指向"扩税基、优结构、稳地方财力 "的目标。 
从"十五五"规划的政策工具设计看，收入侧改革已形成系统化的方向清单：一是提高直 接税
比重，完善个税综合征收；二是健全资本、经营、财产所得税收政策；三是完善增 值 税抵扣
链条与留抵退税；四是消费税征收环节后移并稳步下划地方；五是建立适应新业态的 税收制
度；六是提高国有资本收益收取比例，政府收入全口径入预算；七是规范税收优惠， 保持合
理宏观税负。 
我们认为，财税体制改革的政策逻辑，是用"制度化、可预期的财力分配 "替代"土地财 政这一
不稳定、 非规范的收入来源"。 其宏观含义在于： 地方财政的功能定位从"扩张主体"转向 " 公共
服务与执行主体"，而信用扩张与资本形成的职能则系统性上移至中央。这一 "事权上收 、财
权上移"的再平衡，是理解"十五五"财政运行框架的制度内核，也是中央扩表得以成为 主渠道
的前提条件。扩税基、优结构、稳地方财力，比简单提高宏观税负更重要 ——这是本 轮财税
改革与过去"加税式"改革最本质的区别。 
（二） 中央表内加杠杆为主渠道：超长期特别国债与 "六网一体化"战略的宏
观对冲

### Page 14

请仔细阅读本报告末页声明                                                                                  Page 14 / 19

[Table_Page]  宏观研究  
2026 年 9 月 24 日

在地方扩表能力退潮的背景下，中央表内加杠杆成为稳增长的重要主渠道，其核心工 具是赤
字与超长期特别国债。2025 年赤字率历史性突破 4%，2026 年"十五五"开局进一步将 赤字规
模提升至 5.89 万亿元、赤字率维持在约 4%，且增量全部列于中央本级。超长期特别 国债发
行规模达 1.3 万亿元，专项债维持 4.4 万亿元的高位（见图表 3）。这一组合，构成了 中央财
政对地方财政收缩的宏观对冲，其最直接的证据是 2025 年广义财政支出首次突破 40 万亿
元。 
超长期特别国债的战略意义，不仅在于规模，更在于其期限结构与投向的匹配。 "超 长期" 的
期限设计，对应着科技攻关、重大基础设施、能源安全、水利网络等长回报周期资本 开支的
现金流特征， 避免了传统债务"短债长投"的期限错配风险； 而"特别国债"的主权信用属 性，则
以最低的融资成本为国家战略资产的形成提供资金。 从投向看， 超长期特别国债重点 支持" 两
重"（国家重大战略实施和重点领域安全能力建设）与 "两新"（大规模设备更 新和消费 品以旧
换新） ，并新增支持"六网一体化"战略——即以算力网、能源网、交通网、水利网、 通信 网、
物联感知网为代表的现代基础设施网络的系统集成。  
我们认为，中央表内加杠杆的宏观对冲效应，体现在两个层面：其一，总量层面，中 央信用
的扩张部分对冲了地方信用的收缩，避免总需求出现断崖式下滑；其二，结构层面， 中央信
用的投向聚焦于提升全要素生产率的领域，其资本形成的 "质"远高于旧模式下以土地开 发为
核心的"量"。这一"中央加杠杆、地方防风险"的分工，是"十五五"财政运行区别于以往 任何一
轮周期的核心特征。 
值得强调的是，中央加杠杆的"表内化"是其区别于以往的关键。过去多轮财政扩张中， 相当
比例的信用扩张通过表外的城投、 影子银行、 非标渠道实现， 风险难以穿透、 成本居高 不下；
而本轮中央扩表以赤字、国债、专项债等表内工具为主渠道，其透明度、可监控性与 成本优
势均显著优于旧模式。这一"表内化"的转变，不仅是风险防控的需要，更是信用创造 机制 " 阳
光化、规范化"的制度性进步。 
（三） 固定资产投资（ FAI）的硬核切换：从"铁公基"转向"新科技" 
支出端重置最直观的落点，是固定资产投资（FAI）内涵的"硬核切换" 。旧模式下，固 定资产
投资的引擎是"铁公基"——铁路、 公路、 基建与土地开发， 其原材料消耗强度高、 对大 宗商品
的拉动大，但边际产出效率递减。新模式下，固定资产投资的引擎切换为 "新科技" —— 制造
业技改、设备更新、算力基础设施、新型能源与数字基础设施，其资本开支的指向是 全要素
生产率的提升。 
这一切换在 2025 年的数据中已清晰显现（见图表 5） 。研发经费投入强度升至2.80% 、首次
超过 OECD 平均水平，研发经费支出达 3.93 万亿元、同比+8.1% ；高技术制造业增 加值占
规上工业比重升至 17.1%、增加值同比+9.4%；装备制造业占规上工业比重升至 36.8% ；数
字产品制造业增加值占规上工业比重升至 12.5%、同比+9.3%；工业机器人产量达 7 7.3万
套、同比+28.0%；技术合同成交金额达 7.6 万亿元、同比 +10.8% 。与此同时，传统 地产与
基建投资的增速持续低迷，固定资产投资结构呈现 " 科技升、地产降、基建稳 "的清晰格局。  
我们认为，FAI 的"硬核切换"是财政支出端重置的产业镜像。财政资源的配置重心从 " 补企业 "
转向"补能力、补平台、补首用、分风险"，通过科研经费包干制、央地共担、研发税 制、首台
套保险、耐心资本与国家创业投资引导基金等工具，撬动社会资本投向科技与先进制 造。从
"十五五"规划的政策工具设计看， 科技支持已从"点状的补贴 "升级为"系统化的资本生 态 " ——
基础研究、中试验证、首台套应用、场景开放、政府采购自主创新产品、国家创业投 资引导
基金，构成了从"0 到 1"到"1 到 N"的全链条支持体系。  
（四） 从信用扩张到生产率：新增长模式能否 有效传导

### Page 15

请仔细阅读本报告末页声明                                                                                  Page 15 / 19

[Table_Page]  宏观研究  
2026 年 9 月 24 日

前文所述财政信用结构变化，本质上解决的是"谁来提供新增信用 "以及"新增信用投入 结构转
变"的问题；而对于“十五五”期间经济增长模式而言，更重要的问题则是“新增信用 能否形
成有效回报” 。 如果中央财政承担更多逆周期和战略性投资职能， 但新增资本形成无法 转化为
生产率、企业利润和居民收入，那么财政扩张仍然主要体现为需求端托底，其对潜在 增长率
的提升将受到限制。因此，判断新增长模式是否真正形成，关键是能否形成“财政信 用 —资
本形成—生产率—收入—税基”的有效传导。  
这一新增长模式目前仍处于形成和验证阶段。2025 年研发投入、 高技术制造业和装备 制造业
等指标的改善，为新模式提供了供给侧证据，但从科技资本开支进一步传导至企业盈 利、居
民收入和财政税基，仍需要时间。 “十五五” 期间最值得关注的是财政扩张能否最终形 成可持
续的生产率回报。 
2025 年全员劳动生产率增速达 6.1%、 高于同期GDP 增速（5.0% ）， 这一变化与先进 制造和
科技投资占比提升的方向一致，为观察资本形成效率改善提供了积极信号。未来需要 进一步
观察企业盈利、全要素生产率、居民收入以及财政税基等能否同步改善，只有这些变 量形成
持续反馈，科技投资驱动的新增长模式才真正形成有效回报。  
五、 资本市场长期趋势：基于财政生态重构的资产定价范式迁移 
（一） 固收市场：无风险利率中枢长周期下移与城投债信用利差的趋势性走
阔 
财政生态的重构，首先重塑的是固收市场的定价基准。我们认为， " 十五五" 期间固收市 场将
演绎两条并行的主线：无风险利率中枢的长周期下移，与城投债信用利差的趋势性走阔。  
无风险利率中枢的下移，源自信用扩张主体的切换。当信用扩张的主体从高融资成本 的地方
平台切换到以主权信用融资的中央财政，边际融资成本的系统性下移成为必然。中央 财政以
超长期特别国债与赤字融资， 其利率水平远低于地方平台的历史融资成本， 叠加"低息 借新还
旧"置换高成本债务的持续推进， 全社会无风险利率中枢将呈现长周期、 趋势性的下移 。从数
据看，地方政府债券的平均付息成本持续下行，十年期国债收益率中枢也呈趋势性回 落，这
正是新旧信用锚切换在利率端的映射。利率中枢下移的宏观含义是双重的：一方面， 它降低
了全社会的融资成本，为科技与制造业的资本开支提供了低成本的资金环境；另一方 面，它
也意味着固收资产的"票息"收益系统性下降，传统的 "躺赚"式配置逻辑难以为继。  
与之形成鲜明对比的是，城投债的信用利差将趋势性走阔。过去市场对城投债的定价 ，隐含
了"政府兜底"的信用背书，城投债与国债之间的利差被显著压缩， 甚至一度出现信用风 险与
风险溢价不相匹配的定价错位。但随着" 退平台" 的推进与政府信用与 平台信用的彻底切 割，
城投债的定价将逐步回归其作为企业信用的真实风险水平，信用利差的结构性走阔是 必然趋
势。 
需要特别说明的是，所谓城投债的“风险收益倒挂 ”，并不是指城投债收益率绝对高于企 业债 。
单看收益率水平，风险较高的资产要求更高的收益率，本身属于正常的风险补偿；真 正的定
价错位在于，在地方政府隐性信用较强的时期，部分城投主体自身的资产质量、现金 流和偿
债能力已经弱于同评级产业企业，但其融资成本并未充分反映这一信用差异 ，市场事 实上仍
按照接近地方政府信用的逻辑对其定价 ，即“实际风险上升 、风险溢价却未同步上升 ”。因此 ，
“退平台”和政府信用与平台信用逐步切割之后 ，部分城投债利率上升 、信用利差走阔 ，并不是
新的风险收益倒挂，恰恰是此前隐性担保压低风险溢价之后的正常化重定价。  
我们认为，"主权信用资产"与"地方平台信用资产"的二元分化将正式确立：以国债、超 长期特
别国债为代表的主权信用资产获得"类无风险" 的定价，而城投债则经历从 "类政府债" 到" 高风

### Page 16

请仔细阅读本报告末页声明                                                                                  Page 16 / 19

[Table_Page]  宏观研究  
2026 年 9 月 24 日

险企业债"的重定价过程， 信用利差的分层与走阔将重塑固收市场的配置格局。 这一二 元分化
的投资含义在于：固收配置应系统性向主权信用与高等级资产倾斜，而对于城投债， 则需从
"信仰逻辑"切换到"信用分析逻辑"， 关注地方综合财力、 付息率与再融资能力的实质性 差异—
—前文所述的利息支出率 （青海27%、 甘肃18%） 与宽口径负债率 （甘肃141% 、 浙江1 32%）
的区域分化，正是城投债信用分层定价的基本面依据，而非笼统的 "城投信仰"。 
（二） 权益市场： 财政信用重构下，行业盈利与估值逻辑分化 
财政生态重构对权益市场的冲击更为深远， 其核心是资产定价范式的迁移。 我们认为 ，" 十五
五"期间权益市场最核心的配置命题，是在财政信用重置的背景下，完成从 "土地信用 资产 " 向
"国家信用资产"的定价切换。 
其一，传统 To-G 端资产面临估值体系 的系统性下修。过去二十年，依赖政府订单、 土地开
发链条与基建投资的"T o-G"资产——地产链、传统基建、市政公用、园林环保等 ——其盈 利
与估值长期受益于地方政府的信用扩张。随着地方财政紧平衡常态化（一般公共预算 收入转
负、付息挤占加剧） 、政府固投扩张退出、专项债从"增量投资"退化为"缺口填补"，这些 资产
的收入端与现金流端面临长周期的萎缩，其估值中枢将随之系统性下移。我们认为， 对于 传
统 To-G 端资产面临估值体系系统性下修的压力，其配置价值需结合收入模式转型进 展审慎
评估，其估值的修复需要等待收入模式的根本性转型，而非 简单的周期反弹。这一判 断的核
心依据，在于前文论证的"地方财政长周期缩表 " ——当付息挤占、债务锁定与土地财 政 收缩
三者叠加，To-G 资产的"需求方"（地方政府）的支付能力在长周期内是趋势性走弱的。  
其二，由主权信用背书、承接"新质生产力 "资本开支的科技硬资产与算力基础设施，将 获得
跨越周期的风险溢价重估。中央财政通过超长期特别国债、专项债、国家创业投资引 导基金
与政府采购等工具， 将主权信用注入科技与算力领域的资本开支， 形成了"国家信用背 书＋确
定性资本开支＋政策持续性"的独特组合。 这类资产的估值逻辑， 不再依赖市场化的需求波 动，
而是获得主权信用托底的确定性溢价。具体而言，算力基础设施（智算中心、数据中心 、 AI
算力网络） 、关键装备与核心零部件（工业母机、集成电路、先进材料） 、新型能源体 系（储
能、新型电池、绿氢） 、以及具身智能与机器人等方向，均处于"国家信用资产"重估的 受益区
间。 
我们认为， 这一"规避 To-G、 拥抱国家信用硬资产"的切换， 其本质是权益市场定价基 准从" 土
地信用"向"国家信用"的迁移。随着"十五五"期间中央扩表与科技固投化的持续推进，主 权信
用背书的科技硬资产将成为权益市场跨越周期的主线资产，而这一重估的幅度与持续 性，取
决于"新质生产力"能否如期形成可计量的生产率回报， 进而验证中央加杠杆模式的可持续 性。
这既是"十五五"的投资机遇，也是验证风险 ——若科技资本开支未能及时转化为生产率 与利
润回报，则"国家信用资产"的重估将面临回摆风险，中央加杠杆的可持续性也将受到挑战。  
综合固收与权益两端，财政生态重构对资本市场的定价影响可以归结为一句话：定 价的 锚，
正在从"土地"迁移到"国家信用"。固收端表现为"主权信用利率下行、地方信用利差走 阔 " 的二
元分化，权益端表现为"T o-G 资产下修、科技硬资产重估 "的结构切换。从更长周期 看 ，如果
"中央信用—科技投资—生产率—企业盈利 —财政税 基"的新循环能够逐步形成，那么资 产定
价的基础也将从过去较多依赖土地和地方信用，逐步转向更加关注中央政府信用、企 业现金
流和生产率预期。理解并顺应这一范式迁移，是 "十五五"期间大类资产配置的核心命题 。

风险提示

### Page 17

请仔细阅读本报告末页声明                                                                                  Page 17 / 19

[Table_Page]  宏观研究  
2026 年 9 月 24 日

1、 若房地产与土地市场下行幅度超预期， 政府性基金收入修复迟于预期， 地方财政紧 平衡或
进一步加剧，化债进度与稳增长诉求之间的平衡难度上升；  
2、 财政扩张若仍以结构性投放为主、 总量托底力度不足， 或难以有效对冲需求缺口，价格中
枢低位运行的时间可能拉长； 
3、 科技与算力资本开支若未能及时形成可计量的生产率回报， 中央加杠杆的持续性及 其对私
人部门的挤出效应存在不确定性。

投资评级说明  
投资建议的评级标准 
报告中投资建议所涉及的评级分为股票评级和
行业评级（另有说明的除外）。评级标准为报告
发布日后 6 到 12 个月内的相对市场表现， 也即：
以报告发布日后的 6 到12个月内的公司股价 （或
行业指数） 相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数的
涨跌幅作为基准。 其中：A 股市场以沪深300指
数为基准；香港市场以恒生指数为基准； 美国市
场以纳斯达克综合指数或标普500 指数为基准。 
 评级  说明  
股票
评级 
买入 预期个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数的回报在20%及以上； 
增持 预期个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数的回报介于5%～20%之间； 
持有 预期个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数的回报介于-10%～5%之间； 
卖出 预期个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数的回报在-10%及以下； 
无评级 预期对于个股未来 6 个月市场表现与基准指数相比无明确观点。 
行业
评级 
看好 预期行业整体回报高于基准指数整体水平10%以上； 
中性 预期行业整体回报介于基准指数整体水平-10%～10%之间； 
看淡 预期行业整体回报低于基准指数整体水平-10%以下。

Index is the benchmark index of the U.S. 
market.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The Cotality home value index, covering all of Australia,
fell 1.1% in September with downward revisions to the
previous two months again shaving a further 0.4% off prices.
The correction has now seen prices decline 5.2% from their

March peak. Annual growth is flat.
The price correction remains broad, Cotality reporting 97% of
capital city suburbs experienced falls over the three months
to September, Darwin the only city to record gains. Sydney
and Melbourne continue to see more pronounced declines,
albeit moderating a touch, particularly in Melbourne.
Against this, declines in Brisbane, Adelaide and Perth have
accelerated over the last few months. Across segments, unit
prices and lower price tiers continue to track slightly milder

falls than houses and top tiers.
Seasonal variations accentuated price weakness through winter
but are now neutral. The peak-to-trough price decline nationally
is slightly milder at 4.6% in seasonally adjusted terms. Unless
specified, figures in titles, tables and the commentary below are
in non-seasonally adjusted terms, consistent with the ‘headline’
figures reported by Cotality. However, figures shown in charts
are in seasonally adjusted terms.

Preliminary estimates of turnover show rolling 3mth volumes
down 3.9%qtr in September and 17.4%yr with previous
estimates revised 1-2ppts lower. The monthly pattern
suggests small gains in July and August have given way to
another sizeable fall in September.
Looking at the price performance across different jurisdictions:
• Sydney prices dropped 1.4% fall in September, monthly
declines averaging 1.6% over the previous four months,
tracking a 17% annualised pace of decline since May. All

segments and tiers recorded falls in Q3 but with top and
middle tier houses notably weaker.
• Melbourne prices fell 0.7%mth, a slower pace than other
capitals. This likely reflects the weaker starting point coming
into the correction, prices already back to the levels seen in
mid-2021. As elsewhere, 'top tier' houses prices are leading
the decline. 'Low tier' unit prices mostly held their ground in
Q3, prices in this sub-segment already plumbing 10yr lows.

• Brisbane was the weakest capital city price-wise, recording
a 1.5% drop in September with previous monthly declines
also revised down heavily (August from 1% to 1.6% and
July from 1.2% to 1.7%). The combined effect has taken
the rolling 3mth change to –4.7%qtr from –2.7%qtr last
month and –0.6%qtr in July. At 5.9%yr, annual growth is
still positive but losing altitude quickly, having run at 18%yr
as recently as May. Sellers have been slow to respond to

the sudden stop with total listings up over 50% vs a year

• Adelaide dwelling prices fell 1.3%mth, the August decline

still positive in Darwin (0.4%mth, 11.9%yr), relatively
resilient in Hobart (–0.5%mth, 7%yr) but weaker in
Canberra (–1.1%mth, –1.6%yr).
• Regional markets showed a 0.9% decline in Qld but slightly
milder declines in NSW (–0.7%) and Victoria (0.3%). Annual
prices growth is in the 3.4-6.3%yr range.

Our recently released Housing Pulse report (here) provides
a deeper diver into the situation in Australia's housing
markets. While the absence of selling pressure, still tight
supply-demand balance and sentiment mix suggests the
correction was still relatively 'orderly' through the September
quarter, the latest monthly data shows momentum remains
unambiguously negative.
That said, there are clearly more headwinds to come. The
RBA latest interest rate rise and what now looks to be a

more likely prospect of a follow-up increase at its meeting in
early November will impact in coming months. Next week's
consumer sentiment update will show how the change, and
the latest round of weak updates, is shaping housing-related
sentiment. Either way, momentum looks set to remain weak
into year-end and, barring a significantly improved inflation
and interest rate outlook, early 2027.

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE

2 of 8 September 14, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Superintelligence and Superabundance in the Next Decade
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
While some academics have claimed the Singularity is still off in the distant future, or even
unlikely at all, those on the front lines of AI model development have recently asserted that it
has already begun. For example, Elon Musk has pointed to recent issues with AI models going

outside their proscribed limits, including hacking external systems and completing previously
unsolved math problems. Over a year ago, Sam Altman wrote, "We are past the event horizon; the
takeoff has started. Humanity is close to building superintelligence..." In short, the rapid evolution
of these models has convinced knowledgeable observers that the Singularity has begun.
Superintelligence is the primary driver of the Singularity as exponential advances in AI ultimately

create an artificial Superintelligence that is much smarter and faster than the collective
intelligence of humanity. As such, it can solve practical problems that remain unsolved by humans
and do it in a timely fashion. Superabundance is the logical implication of Superintelligence
as it works to create a world where economic goods, services and energy are essentially free
because of the omniscience implied by the most efficient processing of unlimited information by

superintelligent models. In essence, Superintelligence could mimic the Creation.
Some aspects of Superabundance include automated production of desired output that
eliminates most physical labor, clean energy and synthetic materials that eliminate resource
scarcity, and increased longevity as Superintelligence is applied to maintaining physical health
via, for example, synthetic organ replacement and personalized illness treatments. Obviously,
Superabundance pulls the rug out from under traditional economics, which is the study of how

scarce resources are allocated. While the "scarcity" vanishes in this new world, the allocation issue
remains and is likely to become the focus of political debate. In particular, the question of how
jobs and incomes fit into a superabundant world is likely to become a bigger and bigger issue.
The trend of technology replacing more and more of the labor share of income is already decades
old and dates back to the beginnings of digital technology. That trend has picked up speed

with the increasing profit share of output the new technologies created. The ratio of income
to the number of workers is orders of magnitude higher for the big new technology companies
compared to the old economy companies of the past. This means the owners of capital
(shareholders) are receiving a bigger share of the economic pie as high-margin, high-productivity
companies based in the new technology become a bigger part of the economy. This trend is

accelerating. That is a direct implication of the movement toward and through the Singularity.
It means the issue of income inequality, which has always been on society's radar screen, will
become bigger in the years ahead unless there are wise policies to share the superabundance
of the near future. Some countries are better prepared for this future than others. Norway,
for example, has built a sovereign wealth fund (SWF) over the past 25 years that represents a

substantial share of capital income for each of its citizens. In the U.S., with its widespread equity
culture, thanks to Individual Retirement Accounts and 401K plans, capital income is more widely
distributed compared to Europe with its much more debt-based capital markets. In any event,
widespread equity ownership is one way to deal with a superabundant economy where AI has
significantly reduced the labor share of income.
Demographics add to the natural evolution from labor income to capital income as societies

become wealthier. Aging societies of retirees by definition depend on capital income more than
labor income. This transition of the economy to higher margins and faster earnings growth is
the fortunate accompaniment to the need for more income from capital as societies become
older. The longevity trend naturally accelerates this process, which is the logical result of
technological progress raising productivity and living standards. People live longer in wealthy

societies, as subsistence constraints on life spans diminish while lifestyle and mental health
constraints become more the issue. How lifestyles and mental health evolve in a world where
work becomes less necessary is already showing signs of their increased importance for the
future of public health.
The current accelerating profits cycle reflects this impact of new technology on the relative
returns to labor and capital. The trend so far has been relatively slow compared to what's

coming if Superintelligence and Superabundance are the outcomes the Singularity promises as
increasingly seems likely. Because much financial analysis relies on past cycles the accelerating
Investment Implications
Rapid innovation, political upheaval
and increasing creative destruction
raise the difficulty of valuing
longer duration assets as we saw
earlier this year in the software
space. Higher-risk premia are a
likely result. On balance, this favors

Value-oriented assets over Growth.
Higher real yields in a strong capital
spending environment reinforce this
preference.

### Page 3

3 of 8 September 14, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
profits and rising margins of the current economy have naturally focused attention on what
happens when conditions revert to the mean of lower margins and more historically normal
profit growth. Looked at through this lens, Equities appear overvalued and destined to correct
bubble-like conditions. The Singularity, however, implies "this time is different," the four most
dangerous words in investing. Yet, the slowly evolving shift toward capital income already under

way is an early warning sign of bigger things to come as these trends are turbo-charged to warp
speed while the economy passes through the Singularity. Seen this way, a super-cycle in profits,
margins and stock values seems logical.
A revamping of the social safety net to address the conditions implied by Superabundance
is already a hot topic in Silicon Valley. Various proposals for a universal basic income (UBI)
are being bandied about. Typically, UBI refers to the government giving everyone an income

sufficient to meet some living standard. This automatically implies a big role for government in
the new economy. Given the fourth-turning breakdown in the political process and governance,
alternatives that avoid the government sector might also be considered. For example, another
implication of the Singularity is an accelerating accumulation of wealth fueling a growing supply
of altruistic and philanthropic potential that arguably could be administered more efficiently by

the private sector than the government where widespread allegations of corrupt administration
at all levels have proliferated since the pandemic. Given the growing wealth pie and the diversity
of social priorities among its owners addressing more social needs through philanthropy is a
likely option for the superabundant economy. In any event, the relative roles of government and
the private sector are necessarily an issue to be hashed out in this revolutionary time.

More glimmers of the UBI-like movements have popped up recently. As mentioned, Norway's
SWF is an example of how a well-administered government has been preparing for this future.
In the U.S. government sponsored tax-advantaged savings accounts from birth have been
instituted with private donations being added to the program. Governments, including the U.S.,
have increasingly been buying stakes in private investments for various purposes with the result

that the focus solely on sovereign debt can be a misleading indicator of fiscal fragility.
Japan is a significant example of the difference between gross government debt and net
government debt after subtracting government owned assets. Its gross debt is around 279% of
its gross domestic product (GDP) which is much higher than any other major economy. However,
Japan government assets total around 192% of GDP , including domestic and foreign Equities,

bonds, and loans. As a result, its net debt is less than 100% of GDP and more in line with other
major economies. As one of the most aged societies in the world, Japan points the way toward
how societies can cope with longevity. Government ownership of a broad array of higher yielding
global assets funded with low sovereign debt interest rates appears to be a path the U.S.
Treasury is also starting to pursue in managing its debt burden.
The main risks to the uptopian promise of Superabundance are the dystopian possibilities

that would follow from poor social decision-making. Around the world, different governments
will follow different approaches to navigating the unprecedented challenges posed by the
Singularity. As the U.S. celebrates its 250-year anniversary, it is in the midst of its fourth "fourth
turning." These are the intense political upheaval periods that demographers and historians have
documented in every fourth generation. The Revolutionary War, the Civil War, and the Great

Depression and World War II mark the first three "fourth turnings." The Singularity is a major
marker for the current fourth turning.
The relative success of the U.S. in transitioning through the previous major turning points in
history provides hope for a utopian rather than a dystopian outcome. Other countries have not
transited these challenges so well for such an extended period which helps explain how the
U.S. has become the most prosperous country on earth. Nevertheless, past performance is no

guarantee of future success. As always, each generation faces new challenges to overcome, or not.
For investors, the Singularity seems to be behind an unusually strong earnings cycle that could
potentially last much longer than past history would suggest. That's positive for Equities and
risk assets in general. The extra risks associated with this rapid transformation argue for well
diversified holdings as creative destruction on steroids seems a plausible outcome in a world of

warp speed change.

### Page 4

4 of 8 September 14, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Elevated Debt Supply Is Testing the Market's Appetite
Chief Investment Office, Fixed Income Strategy Team
For much of the past two decades, rising debt levels appeared to carry few
consequences. Federal deficits expanded and corporate borrowing increased, yet
Treasury yields generally trended lower and credit spreads stayed remarkably tame.
Today, the backdrop may be changing.

Federal borrowing needs continue to grow as deficit spending remains elevated. Net
interest expense has surpassed $1 trillion annually and now rivals total U.S. defense
spending. On this front there appears no end in sight as the Congressional Budget
Office projects that the fiscal 2026 shortfall will widen to roughly $2.1 trillion. Investors
are increasingly focused on not only the level of debt outstanding, but also the pace at
which new debt is being issued—with some questioning about the sustainability of this

situation over the longer term.
This phenomenon is not limited to Treasurys, as the issuance glut extends to corporate
debt. Investment-grade (IG) corporate issuance is running roughly 40% above last year
and is on pace for a record year. 1 While demand for Fixed Income remains healthy, the
sheer volume of new debt being brought to market is beginning to test investor capacity,
particularly at the long end of the Treasury curve and within certain sectors.

Exhibit 1: Low Yields Post-2008/2009 GFC* Have Fueled a Borrowing Binge.

through 2026; 2026 reflects the first quarter.

Hyperscalers Are Competing for Long-Duration Capital. A significant driver of
the surge in corporate issuance has been the hyperscalers. The largest technology
companies are financing an unprecedented wave of AI-related capital spending, leading
to nearly $280 billion of debt issuance year-to-date. 2 Many of these issuers carry high
credit ratings and have ramped up issuance of longer-maturity bonds. As a result, they
are increasingly competing with long-dated Treasurys for the same pool of institutional

capital, including insurance companies, pension funds, sovereign wealth funds and
duration-oriented Fixed Income investors—creating a crowding-out effect amongst high-
quality Fixed Income.
Fortunately, this is a case of pressure, not a credit crisis. Recent weakness in long-dated
Treasurys reflects a classic supply-and-demand imbalance and importantly is not a
solvency crisis for the U.S. government or high-quality hyperscalers. Investors are being

asked to absorb a growing volume of net new issuance from both the U.S. Treasury and
corporate America at the same time.
Debt Pressure and a Normalization, Not a Fiscal Reckoning. The rise in long-end
yields during August reflects this dynamic. The 30-year Treasury yield moved above
5.3%, reaching levels not seen since 2007. Fiscal concerns likely played a role, but we
1 Source: Bloomberg as of September 8, 2026.
2 Source: Bloomberg as of September 9, 2026.

Investment Implications
The recent rise in Treasury yields
reflects a collision of fiscal concerns,
resilient growth, persistent inflation,
and record debt issuance from both
the Treasury and corporate borrowers.
While this has created periodic bouts
of market pressures, we view recent
move as a normalization process, not a
crisis of confidence. Attractive real and
nominal yields reinforce our view that
investors should look beyond cash and
short-duration assets and move closer

to strategic duration target.

### Page 5

5 of 8 September 14, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
believe heavy supply, sticky inflation, reduced Fed demand and increased competition for
investor capital were equally important contributors.
And importantly, the market's adjustment mechanism is already working. Higher Treasury
yields and somewhat wider credit spreads are attracting buyers and helping clear the growing
supply of bonds. This is a natural normalization and is how markets restore equilibrium.

Recent Treasury auctions continue to attract demand consistent with historical norms, and
investor appetite for new IG issuance has remained robust. These are not characteristics
typically associated with a loss of confidence in U.S., or corporate, creditworthiness.
A New Yield Regime. For Fixed Income investors, the more important implication
is that a 4% to 5% 10-year Treasury yield may represent the new normal. In an
environment of very strong underlying economic growth and unprecedented volumes of

debt issuance from both the public and private sectors, higher yields may be required to
attract sufficient capital amid a more competitive landscape for yield.
We continue to view both nominal and real yields as attractive and believe investors
should remain focused on achieving strategic duration targets. While rate volatility may
persist, today's yield levels provide a potential opportunity to seek attractive income that
has been largely absent for much of the past decade.

The Bigger Picture: Growth Still Matters More Than Debt Levels. None of this dismisses
the long-term fiscal challenges facing the U.S. Debt levels are rising, interest costs
continue to grow, and mandatory spending increasingly consumes a larger share of the
federal budget. However, history shows that debt levels alone do not determine interest
rates. Between 2007 and 2020, U.S. debt rose dramatically even as the 10-year Treasury
yield fell from roughly 5% to below 1%. What ultimately matters is the interplay between

short-term interest rates, debt levels, economic growth, inflation and investor demand.
For instance, nominal GDP growth and the 10-year yield tend to be correlated, outside
of recessions—we believe recent rate increases partly reflect this strong macro
backdrop. Moreover, economic growth is running above long-end yields, supporting the
sustainability of rising government debt.

Reserve Board; National Bureau of Economic Research. Data as of September 9, 2026.
The U.S. continues to benefit from several structural advantages: the dollar remains
the world's reserve currency, Treasurys remain the foundation of the global financial

system, and U.S. capital markets are still the deepest and most liquid in the world. Those
advantages continue to support demand for U.S. assets and underpin sovereign credit
quality even as borrowing needs grow.
The bottom line, in our view, is that even though the recent rise in long-end yields catches
many headlines, there is no need to panic. Instead, the recent repricing reflects periodic
episodes of debt pressure rather than a fundamental loss of confidence in U.S. credit. With

borrowing demand from both Washington and corporate America likely to remain elevated,
bond prices may continue to serve as the market's release valve. The consequence is likely
a world of structurally higher long-term yields, not a sovereign debt crisis.

### Page 6

6 of 8 September 14, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What are the markets actually telling us?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Officer
When the markets speak loudly we should listen. There are often clues in the internal trends
within the markets that help us determine how far into an economic cycle we are or what drivers
of growth are most impactful. In addition, we can also find clues that help with determining

the micro picture. In other words, what industries are the most likely to benefit, from a
profitability standpoint is concerned, as the growth picture unfolds? Currently, the markets have
a megaphone, in our view. The year-to-date sharp outperformers have been consistent and
should continue to advance. These include commodities, including copper and oil, Emerging
Markets, Japan in the Developed Markets, Small-cap relative to Large-cap shares, Value versus

Growth, and more cyclically-oriented companies versus more defensive. This current consistent
outperformance informs us that we are still early- to mid-cycle in a supply-led expansion. We are
still early in the build out of the supply that is needed for the natural demand that is there. New
infrastructure is needed to handle the new economy that is progressing and quickly innovating.
This infrastructure needs commodities, all forms of energy, battery storage, minerals, metals,

recycled water, gases, chemicals, and all the compute and cloud capacity, including memory
and other components to handle the path toward mass tokenization. Moreover, the buildup of
government debt in the advanced economies as fiscal deficits widen is forcing large institutional
investors (including central banks and other government entities) to diversify their reserve
bases. This has increased the interest in gold holdings. Countries that have a vast amount of

natural resources, especially oil and gas, are in high demand by investors, which is obvious when
examining year-to-date outperformers. The bottom line is that the markets are telling us that we
are still early- to mid-cycle in this massive buildout, which will require a wide collection of hard
assets, Infrastructure (old and new), land, and within Equities, a more diversified approach to
exposure—particularly areas that are more cyclical- and Value-oriented. We expect this view to

extend itself well into the foreseeable future if not years.
In Fixed Income, it may seem more complex, but it is actually easier to understand what the
markets are telling us, in our opinion. The backup in yields is “real yield” driven, which is more due
to higher nominal growth than to cconcerns of long-run inflation. This is evident in the Treasury
inflation protection market, break-evens in general and the ratio of Treasury yields relative to

other Government bond markets in the developed world. However, rising oil and gas prices and
inflation gauges that are still above the preferred level are usually the excuses for yields backing
up. Deficit concerns are a more plausible reason for yields rising but the main driver, in our view,
is still above average nominal growth. The yield curve and rates in general are still adjusting to
this new economic landscape that is very different than those of the most recent past.

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

Bloomberg Commodity Index designed to provide liquid and diversified exposure to commodities via futures contracts. Is calculated on an excess return basis and reflects commodity
futures price movements.
MSCI Emerging Markets Index captures large and mid cap representation across 24 Emerging Markets countries. With 1,205 constituents, the index covers approximately 85% of the free
float adjusted market capitalization in each country.
MSCI Japan Index is designed to measure the performance of the large and mid cap segments of the Japanese market.

MSCI Pacific ex Japan Index captures large and mid cap representation across 4 of 5 Developed Markets (DM) countries in the Pacific region (excluding Japan). With 93 constituents, the index
covers approximately 85% of the free float-adjusted market capitalization in each country.
MSCI All-Country World Index is a premier benchmark representing global equity performance, covering large and mid-cap stocks across 23 developed and 24 emerging markets.

MSCI World Index ex U.S. captures large and mid cap representation across 22 of 23 Developed Markets countries--excluding the United States..
MSCI USA Momentum Index is based on MSCI USA Index, its parent index, which captures large and mid-cap stocks of the U.S. market. It is designed to reflect the performance of an equity
momentum strategy by emphasizing stocks with high price momentum, while maintaining reasonably high trading liquidity, investment capacity and moderate index turnover.

U.S. Large-cap Growth/Russell 1000 Growth Index measures the performance of the large-cap growth segment of the U.S. equity universe. It includes those Russell 1000 companies with
relatively higher price-to-book ratios, higher I/B/E/S forecast medium term (2 year) growth and higher sales per share historical growth (5 years).
U.S. Small-cap/Russell 2000 Index measures the performance of the small-cap segment of the U.S. Equity universe.
MSCI USA High Dividend Yield Index is designed to reflect the performance of equities in the MSCI USA Index (excluding REIT s) with higher dividend income and quality characteristics than

average dividend yields that are both sustainable and persistent.
U.S. Mid Cap/Russell Midcap Index measures the performance of the mid-cap segment of the U.S. Equity universe. The Russell Midcap Index is a subset of the Russell 1000® Index. It
includes approximately 800 of the smallest securities based on a combination of their market cap and current index membership.
MSCI USA Value Index captures large and mid-cap U.S. securities exhibiting overall value style characteristics.

MSCI USA Equal Weighted Index represents an alternative weighting scheme to its market cap weighted parent index, the MSCI USA Index. At each quarterly rebalance date, all index
constituents are weighted equally, effectively removing the influence of each constituent’s current price.
MSCI USA Minimum Volatility Index aims to reflect the performance characteristics of a minimum variance strategy applied to the large and mid-cap USA equity universe.

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA, giá mục 
tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu

2Q2026, AST ghi nhận doanh thu và LNST đạt 479/92 tỷ VND (+22%/19% yoy), với 
động lực chủ yếu đến từ (1) đóng góp từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn 
Nhất và (2) tăng trưởng tích cực của các mảng hàng miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood 
và phòng chờ thương gia. Lũy kế 6T2026, doanh thu và LNST đạt 1,035/221 tỷ VND 
(+33%/46% yoy), lần lượt hoàn thành 48%/49% kế hoạch năm.

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượng khách quốc tế/nội địa tại các thị trường AST khai thác 
tăng bình quân 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030. Lưu lượng và tỷ trọng khách quốc tế 
gia tăng được kỳ vọng thúc đẩy số giao dịch và doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi 
khách, đặc biệt tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

Chúng tôi dự phóng số điểm kinh doanh AST tăng từ 145 điểm cuối năm 2026 lên 
182 điểm năm 2030. Sau đóng góp từ 19 điểm tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất và 6 điểm mới tại 
T2 Nội Bài từ 2025 - 2026, các dự án Long Thành, Phú Quốc và Gia Bình tiếp tục mở 
ra dư địa mở rộng mạng lưới trong trung và dài hạn cho doanh nghiệp.

Việc mở rộng các hoạt động mang lại lợi nhuận cao, bao gồm hàng miễn thuế, 
phòng chờ thương gia và tối ưu hoạt động tại các điểm kinh doanh được kỳ vọng 
tác động tích cực lên biên lợi nhuận của AST. Chúng tôi dự phóng biên lợi nhuận 
gộp đạt 64.7%/65.5% trong năm 2026/2027, hỗ trợ LNST công ty mẹ tăng lần lượt 
48%/17% yoy.

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với AST, giá mục tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương 
đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 21/09/2026.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng không Taseco là một 
trong những doanh nghiệp có quy mô mạng 
lưới điểm kinh doanh lớn trong lĩnh vực dịch 
vụ phi hàng không tại Việt Nam, với 141 
điểm kinh doanh tại 6 sân bay quốc tế tính 
đến cuối tháng 6/2026. Hoạt động cốt lõi của 
công ty bao gồm bán lẻ bách hóa lưu niệm 
(BHLN), hàng miễn thuế, dịch vụ ăn uống và 
phòng chờ thương gia. Ngoài ra, AST còn 
tham gia lĩnh vực suất ăn hàng không thông 
qua khoản đầu tư tại công ty liên kết VinaCS. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Doanh thu trên các điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu tăng nhờ lượng khách quốc tế/nội địa dự phóng tăng 
11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030.

Mở rộng mạng lưới tạo động lực tăng trưởng mới, với số điểm kinh doanh AST 2026 dự kiến đạt 145 
điểm và tăng lên 182 điểm vào 2030. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Biên lợi nhuận gộp cải thiện lên 64.7%/65.5% trong năm 2026/2027 nhờ mở rộng hàng miễn thuế, 
phòng chờ và tối ưu vận hành.

HOẠT ĐỘNG KINH DOANH 
KQKD tăng trưởng tích cực trong 
2Q2026

2Q2026, AST ghi nhận doanh thu và LNST đạt 479/92 tỷ VND (+22%/19% yoy), 
với động lực: (1) đóng góp từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất 
và (2) tăng trưởng tích cực của các mảng hàng miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood 
và phòng chờ thương gia. Lũy kế 6T2026, doanh thu và LNST đạt 1,035/221 tỷ 
VND (+33%/46% yoy), lần lượt hoàn thành 48%/49% kế hoạch năm.

Biểu đồ 5. Doanh thu/điểm kinh doanh của AST

Nguồn:AST

Tăng trưởng khách qua cảng hàng 
không thúc đẩy doanh thu tại các 
điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng tăng trưởng lưu lượng hành khách, đặc biệt là sự gia tăng tỷ 
trọng khách quốc tế, sẽ thúc đẩy doanh thu và hiệu quả kinh doanh của AST 
trong giai đoạn 2026–2030. Theo Cục Hàng không Việt Nam, lượng khách thông 
qua các cảng hàng không trong 7T2026 đạt hơn 76 triệu lượt (+7% yoy), trong 
đó khách quốc tế đạt gần 30 triệu lượt (+11% yoy), trong khi khách nội địa ghi 
nhận tăng trưởng 4% yoy. Xu hướng này được hỗ trợ bởi nhu cầu du lịch quốc 
tế, chính sách thị thực thuận lợi và việc mở rộng mạng bay, trong khi tăng 
trưởng kinh tế và thu nhập tiếp tục tạo nền tảng cho nhu cầu đi lại trong nước. 
Trên cơ sở đó, chúng tôi dự phóng lượt khách quốc tế và nội địa tại các thị 
trường AST khai thác tăng bình quân lần lượt 11% và 5%/năm đến 2030, qua đó 
nâng dần tỷ trọng khách quốc tế trong tổng lưu lượng, thúc đẩy doanh 
thu/điểm kinh doanh nhờ: (1) lưu lượng hành khách qua cảng hàng không gia 
tăng và (2) tỷ trọng khách quốc tế cao hơn hỗ trợ doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi 
khách, đặc biệt tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

lượt) 
 Biểu đồ 7. Doanh thu/điểm kinh doanh AST (tỷ VND)

Nguồn: AST  Nguồn: AST

Mở rộng mạng lưới tại các nhà ga 
hiện hữu và sân bay mới, gia tăng 
quy mô kinh doanh đến 2030 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng chu kỳ mở rộng hạ tầng hàng không giai đoạn 2026–2030 sẽ 
tạo dư địa gia tăng quy mô kinh doanh cho AST, với số điểm kinh doanh dự 
phóng đạt 145 điểm vào cuối 2026 và tăng lên 182 điểm vào năm 2030. Cụ thể: 
- Trong ngắn hạn, tăng trưởng năm 2026 được hỗ trợ bởi việc ghi nhận 
đủ năm doanh thu từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất 
trong năm 2025, cùng 6 điểm mới tại T2 Nội Bài trong năm 2026, trong 
đó có 2 cửa hàng miễn thuế và 2 cửa hàng Memories of Vietnam khai 
trương trong 2Q2026. 
- Trong trung và dài hạn, các dự án Cảng HKQT Long Thành, Cảng HKQT 
Phú Quốc nâng cấp mở rộng và Cảng HKQT Gia Bình sẽ góp phần thúc 
đẩy ngành hàng không Việt Nam nói chung, bổ sung mặt bằng thương 
mại, mở ra cơ hội phát triển các mảng kinh doanh cốt lõi cho AST nói 
riêng bao gồm các mảng bán lẻ, miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood và 
phòng chờ thương gia. Với kinh nghiệm khai thác 141 điểm kinh doanh 
tại 6 sân bay quốc tế và lịch sử trúng thầu tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất, T2 Nội 
Bài trong thời gian qua, chúng tôi kỳ vọng AST có khả năng cạnh tranh 
cao trong việc đấu thầu quyền khai thác mặt bằng tại các sân bay mới, 
đặc biệt là Gia Bình, qua đó duy trì vị thế và thị phần kinh doanh phi 
hàng không tại khu vực phía Bắc khi một phần lưu lượng hành khách 
được phân bổ lại từ Nội Bài.

Cơ cấu kinh doanh và hiệu quả vận 
hành hỗ trợ mở rộng biên lợi nhuận  
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng biên lợi nhuận của AST tiếp tục cải thiện trong 2026 - 2027, 
nhờ định hướng mở rộng các mảng có biên lợi nhuận tốt như hàng miễn thuế 
và phòng chờ thương gia, kết hợp tối ưu hiệu quả vận hành tại các điểm kinh 
doanh. Cụ thể: (1) AST ưu tiên gia tăng số điểm kinh doanh hàng miễn thuế 
nhằm đón đầu tăng trưởng nhanh và ổn định của lượng khách quốc tế, qua đó 
nâng đóng góp lợi nhuận từ hoạt động mua sắm tại sân bay, (2) mở rộng mạng 
lưới phòng chờ, đồng thời gia tăng lượng khách sử dụng trên nền năng lực phục 
vụ hiện hữu và (3) tối ưu hóa hiệu quả hoạt động thông qua cải thiện bố trí mặt 
bằng, danh mục sản phẩm và kiểm soát chi phí tại các điểm kinh doanh, qua 
đó tăng doanh thu và lợi nhuận thu được trên mỗi khách hàng.

Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA, giá 
mục tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu AST, với giá mục tiêu 76,500 
VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 
21/09/2026, dựa trên triển vọng tăng trưởng từ lượng khách quốc tế, mở rộng 
mạng lưới điểm kinh doanh và khả năng cải thiện biên lợi nhuận của doanh 
nghiệp. Chúng tôi sử dụng phương pháp định giá từng phần (SOTP), bao gồm 
DCF cho hoạt động kinh doanh cốt lõi của AST và P/E mục tiêu 11x đối với phần 
lợi nhuận thuộc sở hữu AST tại VinaCS. Các giả định chính gồm: (1) lượng khách 
quốc tế/nội địa tăng trung bình 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030, (2) số điểm kinh 
doanh tăng từ 145 điểm cuối 2026 lên 182 điểm năm 2030, với giả định AST 
trúng thầu mặt bằng tại cả Long Thành và Gia Bình và (3) biên lợi nhuận gộp 
2026/2027 đạt 64.7/65.5%. Đối với VinaCS, chúng tôi kỳ vọng nhà máy suất ăn 
Long Thành sẽ hỗ trợ tăng trưởng lợi nhuận trong trung và dài hạn khi dự kiến 
có thể bắt đầu đóng góp lợi nhuận cho AST từ 2028.

Rủi ro đối với định giá bao gồm: (1) AST không giành được quyền khai thác tại 
Long Thành và Gia Bình như kỳ vọng, (2) lưu lượng khách dịch chuyển giữa các 
sân bay mới và hiện hữu khiến doanh thu tại một số điểm kinh doanh bị sụt 
giảm, (3) cạnh tranh gia tăng từ các doanh nghiệp kinh doanh dịch vụ phi hàng 
không khác và (4) căng thẳng địa chính trị kéo dài, giá dầu tăng gây áp lực lên 
hoạt động hàng không và chi tiêu của hành khách.

History suggests that the euro’s correlation with eurozone
spread widening can pick up rapidly. Today’s budget
announcement in France leaves OATs at risk of further
underperformance, and a so far resilient EUR/USD may be
looking at material downside risks if things don’t improve.

FX spillover from France is starting
This morning's market chatter is increasingly focused on widening
eurozone spreads ﬁnally spilling over into the euro, with the French-
German 10-year spread reaching 130bp.
Both EUR/USD and EUR/CHF have moved lower today. The latter is
providing the clearest signal that bond market stress is feeding into
FX. The dollar continues to beneﬁt from broad-based support,
driven by tighter global ﬁnancial conditions, elevated energy prices

Our colleagues analyse today’s budget announcement in France in
this note. ING’s view is that this budget buys some time but fails to
solve structural deﬁcit issues. Markets still have little incentive to
price ﬁscal premia out of OATs, and with no party having presented
a suﬃciently detailed spending cut plan, the upcoming elections

are adding to the uncertainty. Our rates team believes 150bp may
be the next step for the 10yr OAT-Bund spread.
That creates material downside risks for the euro. Rising yield
volatility and the prospect of wider spreads are pushing the market
back into an environment where the euro becomes much more
sensitive to the ﬁscal and bond narrative. We have seen similar
episodes before, often centred on Italy. Yet the euro's response has
so far been remarkably muted compared with those earlier periods.

In the chart below, we highlight (orange bars) episodes when the
average 10-year spread of France, Spain and Italy versus Germany
widened by at least four standard deviations relative to its six-
month mean. The light blue line shows the deviation of EUR/USD
from its short-term fair value, i.e. the risk premium on the euro. In
most of the other instances of sharp spread-widening, the euro
suﬀered from much more idiosyncratic pressure. For now, that

EUR downside risks increasing
That is mostly bad news for EUR/USD, with a couple of caveats. The
negative aspect is that there is still plenty of room to catch up with
the ﬁscal story. The chart above suggests a 3% risk premium
buildup on the euro would be entirely in line with previous history,
meaning a move to 1.110 is a possibility in a scenario of further
spread widening, even if not extreme.
The positive caveat is that the limited EUR reaction may suggest

that markets are not as concerned as they were about the Italian
ﬁscal trajectory. The US is admittedly facing its own deal of ﬁscal
scrutiny on top of that, and part of the French spread widening has
surely followed higher energy prices, which as we know can fall
quite rapidly in an improved geopolitical landscape.
EUR/CHF is historically the cleanest way to hedge the eurozone
ﬁscal risk, and we could see further downside there too. Still, the
franc’s idiosyncratic fragilities may get in the way, meaning we

don’t expect CHF to outperform USD materially purely on the back
of the French story. A drop to 1.110 in EUR/USD could equal a move
to roughly 0.920-0.925 in EUR/CHF.
